Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of AI-generated visual media has created an urgent need for efficient, trustworthy deepfake detection systems. However, existing deep learning-based detection methods rely on computationally intensive and energy-demanding inference algorithms, limiting their scalability. Here, we present a hybrid digital-analog deepfake video detection framework that combines a lightweight digital front-end with a spatially multiplexed optical decoding back-end for massively parallel analog inference through a programmable spatial light modulator. By simultaneously processing 15 or more video streams within a single optical propagation pass, the system enables high-throughput and accurate video-level authenticity prediction at reduced computational cost compared with purely digital methods. We validated this hybrid deepfake video processor using different datasets spanning classical face-swapping, real-world deepfake recordings, and fully AI-generated videos. Using a spatially multiplexed experimental set-up operating in the visible spectrum, we achieved average deepfake detection accuracy, sensitivity and specificity of 97.79%, 99.86% and 95.72%, respectively, on the Celeb-DF video dataset with 15 videos tested in parallel in a single optical pass per inference. The multiplexed optical decoder also demonstrates resilience against various types of video degradation, noise, compression, experimental misalignments and black-box adversarial attacks. Our results show that integrating optical computation into AI inference enables simultaneous gains in throughput, energy efficiency, and adversarial robustness - three properties that are difficult to achieve together in purely digital systems.

</details>


### [26] [Multi-Scale Generative Modeling with Heat Dissipation Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19371)
*Jun Ma,Hanquan Zhang,Yanjun Qin,Haoyuan Guan,Ke Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Diffusion models are widely used in image generation, with most relying on noise-based corruption and denoising. A distinct branch instead uses blur as the main corruption, preserving better color budgets and multi-scale detail by providing multi-scale priors. However, blur-based models remain in SDE-based frameworks and are not integrated into ODE-based frameworks, such as Flow Matching (FM). Meanwhile, in the blur-based formulation, the classical inverse heat-dissipation (IHD) process faces an ill-posed challenge. Moreover, under the data-manifold assumption, regressing blurred images from high-dimensional noise (or velocity) space is also difficult. We propose Heat Dissipation Flow Matching (HDFM), which introduces a continuous blurred (heat-dissipation) process into FM to inject multi-scale priors. HDFM aligns an interpolated heat-dissipation path to address ill-posedness and adopts $x$-prediction to mitigate high-dimensional regression difficulty. Toy experiments and ablation studies show that HDFM consistently benefits from both blur and $x$-prediction. The performance of HDFM outperforms most baseline methods on all datasets.

</details>


### [27] [Concept-Guided Noisy Negative Suppression for Zero-Shot Classification and Grounding of Chest X-Ray Findings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19374)
*Chenyu Lian,Hong-Yu Zhou,Chun-Ka Wong,Jing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoNNS是一个概念引导的噪声负例抑制框架，通过构建分层概念本体来改进胸部X射线和放射学报告的多模态对齐，解决标准对比学习中不同患者间相似发现被视为负例的噪声问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准对比学习将不同患者的X射线和报告简单视为负对，但不同患者经常表现出相似的发现，这引入了噪声负例。这些噪声负例导致语义模糊，并降低了零样本理解任务的性能。

Method: 1. 使用大语言模型构建包含41个关键临床概念的分层概念本体，明确建模存在性、属性（位置和特征）和文本（证据段落和存在陈述）。
2. 实施跨患者对重标记策略，包括三个步骤：细粒度分类、噪声负例过滤和难负例挖掘。
3. 提出概念感知NCE损失函数，在对齐视觉特征与文本时抑制已识别的噪声负例。

Result: 在多粒度零样本接地任务和五个零样本分类数据集上的广泛实验验证了CoNNS优于现有的最先进模型。

Conclusion: 通过构建结构化的临床概念本体和噪声负例抑制机制，CoNNS显著改善了胸部X射线与放射学报告的对齐质量，提升了零样本分类和接地任务的性能，为解决多模态医学图像分析中的噪声负例问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language alignment using chest X-rays and radiology reports has emerged as an advanced paradigm for zero-shot classification and grounding of chest X-ray findings. However, standard contrastive learning typically treats radiographs and reports from different patients simply as negative pairs. This assumption introduces noisy negatives, as different patients frequently exhibit similar findings. Such noisy negatives cause semantic ambiguity and degrade performance in zero-shot understanding tasks. To address this challenge, we propose CoNNS, a concept-guided noisy-negative suppression framework. To support the negative suppression mechanism, unlike previous methods that use raw reports or templatized texts, we construct a hierarchical concept ontology using large language models. The ontology structures 41 key clinical concepts by explicitly modeling presence, attributes (location and characteristics), and texts (evidential segment and presence statement). Leveraging this ontology, we implement a cross-patient pair relabeling strategy comprising three steps: (1) Fine-Grained Breakdown to categorize pairs based on finding presence; (2) Noisy Negative Filtering to resolve semantic conflicts by removing false negatives; and (3) Hard Negative Mining to identify subtle attribute discrepancies using a lightweight language model. Finally, we propose a Concept-Aware NCE loss to align visual features with text while suppressing the identified noisy negatives. Extensive experiments across multi-granularity zero-shot grounding tasks and five zero-shot classification datasets validate that CoNNS outperforms existing state-of-the-art models. The code is available at https://github.com/DopamineLcy/conns.

</details>


### [28] [MatPhys: Learning Material-Aware Physics Parameters for Deformable Object Simulation from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19386)
*Yang Yang,Yiyan Wang,Zheming Liu,Naoya Iwamoto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MatPhys是一个材料感知的前馈框架，从单视角视频预测弹簧-质量参数，通过材料分解和共享材料代码库解决现有方法材料同质假设和跨场景不一致的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重建模拟就绪的可变形对象在视觉、图形学和机器人学中很重要。现有物理驱动方法可以从视频恢复物理数字孪生，但有两个基本限制：假设整个对象材料同质；场景特定的逆优化与单目观测的固有模糊性导致同一材料在不同场景中参数不一致。

Method: 1. 使用DINO特征将对象分解为语义上有意义的部分，查询部分级材料先验，为每个部分分配其自身的物理行为
2. 引入学习的材料代码库作为外观和物理之间的桥梁
3. 使用部分级先验作为参考分布约束解码器，确保同一材料在不同场景中产生一致的参数

Result: 实验表明，该方法在重建和未来预测方面匹配每场景优化基线，同时对未见过的交互和对象具有更强的泛化能力，物理参数更加一致

Conclusion: MatPhys通过材料感知设计将欠约束的单目问题转化为基于共享、可重用材料概念的前馈推断，实现了跨场景一致的物理参数预测

Abstract: Reconstructing simulation-ready deformable objects is important for vision, graphics, and robotics. Existing physics-driven methods can recover physical digital twins from videos, but they suffer from two fundamental limitations: they typically assume a homogeneous material across the whole object, and their scene-specific inverse optimization, combined with the inherent ambiguity of monocular observation, yields inconsistent parameters for the same material across different scenes or interactions. We propose MatPhys, a material-aware feed-forward framework that predicts spring-mass parameters from a single-view video, addressing these two issues with two coupled designs. To relax the homogeneous material assumption, we use DINO features to decompose the object into semantically meaningful parts and to query a part-level material prior, assigning each part its own physical behavior. To enforce cross-scene consistency, we introduce a learned material codebook of shared material embeddings as the bridge between appearance and physics, and further use the part-level prior as a reference distribution that constrains the decoder so that the same material yields consistent parameters across scenes and interactions. Together, these designs turn an under-constrained monocular problem into feed-forward inference grounded on shared, reusable material concepts. Experiments show that our method matches per-scene optimization baselines in reconstruction and future prediction, while achieving stronger generalization to unseen interactions and objects with more consistent physical parameters.

</details>


### [29] [LMM-Track4D: Eliciting 4D Dynamic Reasoning in LMMs via Trajectory-Grounded Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19390)
*Chaoyue Li,Yongxue Xu,Jie Feng,Jiayu Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种轨迹基础的多轮时空对话任务和Track4D-Bench基准测试，并开发了LMM-Track4D模型，通过显式动态状态建模增强大型多模态模型的4D动态推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的大型多模态模型虽然在图像和视频理解方面能力不断增强，但在维持4D连续时空动态推理方面仍然存在困难。需要专门的任务和基准来研究这一能力差距。

Method: 提出了轨迹基础的多轮时空对话任务，并构建了Track4D-Bench基准测试（包含526个剪辑级对话样本）。在此基础上开发了LMM-Track4D模型，该模型结合了RTGE（射线-时间几何编码）、用于长时动态传播的专用流状态令牌TRK，以及用于在遮挡和视点变化下稳定进行4步3D状态估计的OSK-RA（对象槽运动学-残差锚点）解码器。

Result: 在Track4D-Bench上的实验显示，LMM-Track4D相对于强基线模型取得了持续的改进，表明显式动态状态建模是激发大型多模态模型中4D动态推理能力的有用设计原则。

Conclusion: 通过提出新的任务、基准测试和专门设计的LMM-Track4D模型，本研究证明了显式动态状态建模能够有效提升大型多模态模型的4D时空动态推理能力，为解决现有模型在这一领域的能力差距提供了解决方案。

Abstract: Recent large multimodal models (LMMs) have become increasingly capable on image and video understanding, yet still struggle to sustain 4D continuous spatiotemporal dynamic reasoning. To study this capability gap, we formulate trajectory-grounded multi-turn spatiotemporal dialogue, a new task in which a model must answer spatiotemporal queries while returning structured 3D target trajectories over an entire short clip or a specified segment of a longer clip, and introduce Track4D-Bench, a benchmark with 526 clip-level dialogue samples spanning 23.5k frames and 7.5k object annotations, for training and evaluation. Building on this task, we propose LMM-Track4D, which combines RTGE (Ray--Time Geometry Encoding), a dedicated streaming state token TRK for long-horizon dynamic propagation, and an Object-Slot Kinematic, Residual-Anchor (OSK-RA) decoder for stable 4-step 3D state estimation under occlusion and viewpoint variation. Experiments on Track4D-Bench show consistent improvements over strong baselines, suggesting that explicit dynamic state modeling is a useful design principle for eliciting 4D dynamic reasoning in LMMs. Our code and dataset will be publicly available at https://github.com/mikubaka88/LMM-Track4D.

</details>


### [30] [Neuron Incidence Redistribution for Fairness in Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19393)
*Abin Shoby,Lyle John Palmer,Nikhil Cherian Kurian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种名为神经元发生率重新分配(NIR)的轻量正则化方法，通过惩罚倒数第二层神经元激活的预测概率加权均值方差，无需训练时的人口统计标签，有效减少医学图像分类模型在不同人口亚组间的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习医学图像分类模型在不同人口统计属性（如年龄、性别、种族）亚组间存在性能差异。研究发现这种差异的潜在机制：在迁移学习模型中，正预测时主导的倒数第二层激活通道同时被疾病阳性样本和特权人口组（男性、老年患者）共同激活，导致过度诊断；而负预测时的主导通道则被弱势组（女性、年轻患者）共同激活，导致系统性诊断不足。

Method: 提出神经元发生率重新分配(NIR)，一种轻量正则化方法。该方法惩罚倒数第二层神经元激活的预测概率加权均值方差，从而将潜在的疾病证据分布到整个倒数第二层，而非集中在少数神经元上。该方法无需训练时的人口统计标签。

Result: 在HAM10000数据集上，NIR将年龄组间的TPR差异从10.81%降低到0.93%，性别组间的TPR差异从12.04%降低到0.74%，同时AUC有0.51个百分点的边际提升。在Harvard OCT-RNFL数据集上，NIR将种族间的FPR差异从15.68%降低到10.66%，年龄组间的FPR差异从12.69%降低到1.80%。

Conclusion: 将潜在的疾病证据分布到整个倒数第二层是一种有原则且有效的策略，可以显著改善医学人工智能的人口统计公平性，而无需依赖训练时的人口统计标签。NIR方法简单轻量，在多个数据集上证明了对减少亚组性能差异的有效性。

Abstract: Deep learning models for medical image classification are susceptible to subgroup performance disparities across demographic attributes such as age, gender, and race. We identify a latent representational mechanism underlying these disparities: in transfer-learned models, the dominant penultimate-layer activation channel under positive predictions is co-activated by both disease-positive samples and privileged demographic groups (male, older patients), producing over-diagnosis; conversely, the dominant channel under negative predictions is co-activated by disadvantaged groups (female, younger patients), producing systematic under-diagnosis. To address this, we propose Neuron Incidence Redistribution (NIR), a lightweight regularization method that penalizes the variance of predicted-probability-weighted mean activations across penultimate-layer neurons, requiring no demographic labels at training time. On HAM10000, TPR disparity drops from 10.81% to 0.93% across age groups and from 12.04% to 0.74% across gender, with a marginal AUC improvement of 0.51 points. On Harvard OCT-RNFL, NIR reduces FPR disparity for race (from 15.68% to 10.66%) and age (from 12.69% to 1.80%), demonstrating that distributing latent disease evidence across the full penultimate layer is a principled and effective strategy for improving demographic fairness in medical AI.

</details>


### [31] [Rebalancing Reference Frame Dominance to Improve Motion in Image-to-Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19398)
*Wooseok Jeon,Seungho Park,Seunghyun Shin,Sangeyl Lee,Hyeonho Jeong,Hae-Gon Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DyMoS是一种训练免费、模型无关的方法，通过重新平衡生成帧到参考帧的注意力路径，解决图像到视频模型生成视频过于静态的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像到视频模型通常生成的视频过于静态，相比文本到视频模型缺乏动态性。现有方法通过减弱或修改图像条件信号来缓解这一问题，但往往需要额外训练或牺牲参考图像的保真度。

Method: 作者识别出"参考帧主导"是抑制运动的关键机制，发现非参考帧在I2V模型中过度分配自注意力给参考帧的关键令牌，导致参考信息在时间上过度传播并抑制帧间动态。DyMoS方法在初始去噪步骤中重新平衡从生成帧到参考帧的注意力路径，该方法不需要改变输入图像或模型权重，仅引入单个标量参数来连续控制运动强度。

Result: 在多个最先进的I2V骨干模型上的实验表明，DyMoS一致地改善了运动动态，同时保持了视觉质量和参考图像的保真度。

Conclusion: DyMoS是一种有效的方法，能够解决图像到视频模型中存在的运动抑制问题，通过重新平衡注意力机制来增强视频动态性，而无需额外训练或牺牲图像保真度。

Abstract: Image-to-video models often generate videos that remain overly static, compared to text-to-video models. While prior approaches mitigate this issue by weakening or modifying the image-conditioning signal, they often require additional training or sacrifice fidelity to the reference image. In this work, we identify \emph{reference-frame dominance} as a key mechanism behind motion suppression. We observe that non-reference frames in I2V models allocate excessive self-attention to reference-frame key tokens, causing reference information to be over-propagated across time and suppressing inter-frame dynamics. Based on this finding, we propose DyMoS~(Dynamic Motion Slider), a training-free and model-agnostic method that rebalances the attention pathway from generated frames to the reference frame during initial denoising steps. DyMoS leaves both the input image and model weights unchanged and introduces a single scalar parameter for continuous control over motion strength. Experiments across multiple state-of-the-art I2V backbones demonstrate that DyMoS consistently improves motion dynamics while maintaining visual quality and fidelity to the reference image.

</details>


### [32] [Vision Harnessing Agent for Open Ad-hoc Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19410)
*Zilin Wang,Stella X. Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VASA是一种无需训练、基于视觉引导的智能体，用于开放临时概念分割。它通过视觉工作流程整合VLM智能体、分割基础模型和视觉引导机制，使用持续工作掩码进行推理、构建和验证，在多个基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分割方法对于开放临时概念的分割存在局限性：当概念未作为预学习掩码存在时，需要通过图像证据（如部件、关系、排除和集合）进行构建。当前方法主要依赖文本提示修订，缺乏有效的视觉推理和构建能力。

Method: 提出VASA（Vision-guided Ad-hoc Segmentation Agent），这是首个用于开放临时概念分割的视觉利用智能体。方法特点：1) 无需训练；2) 结合VLM智能体、分割基础模型和视觉引导工作流程；3) 使用持续工作掩码进行推理；4) 能够规划视觉操作、调用分割工具、检查结果、编辑掩码并从错误中恢复。

Result: 在PARS基准测试（基于PartImageNet构建的开放临时概念分割基准）上，VASA相比开放词汇、基于推理和智能体基线方法显著提升，超越SAM3 Agent 14-25%。在RefCOCOm基准测试（标准多粒度参考分割基准）上，超越SAM3 Agent 5-9%，超越其他智能体基线高达20%。

Conclusion: VASA通过视觉构建智能体方法在开放临时概念分割任务上取得了突破性进展。研究表明，AI智能体不应仅仅将基础模型作为工具包装，而应通过任务知识、VLM行为、视觉例程、工作记忆和容错工作流程进行"编程"，这为AI智能体的发展指明了新方向。

Abstract: Segmentation has become easy when the concept is known, requiring retrieval of a learned visual grounding from text. It remains hard for open ad-hoc concepts, where the grounding may not exist as one learned mask and must often be constructed from image evidence through parts, relations, exclusions, and collections. We propose a Vision-guided Ad-hoc Segmentation Agent (VASA), the first vision harnessing agent for open ad-hoc segmentation. VASA is training-free and couples a VLM agent, a segmentation foundation model, and a visually grounded workflow. Rather than revising text prompts alone, VASA uses a persistent working mask to reason, construct, and validate a solution. It plans visual operations, invokes segmentation tools, inspects results, edits the mask, and recovers from errors. We construct PARS, a new benchmark that turns part-level labels in PartImageNet into open ad-hoc concepts through long-form definition queries. On PARS, VASA outperforms open-vocabulary, reasoning-based, and agentic baselines, surpassing SAM3 Agent by 14-25%. On RefCOCOm, a standard multi-granularity referring segmentation benchmark, VASA improves over SAM3 Agent by 5-9% and over other agentic baselines by up to 20%. These results validate agentic visual construction for open ad-hoc segmentation. Our work points to a path for AI agents beyond wrapping foundation models as tools: Programming them with task knowledge, VLM behavior, visual routines, working memory, and failure-aware workflows.

</details>


### [33] [Targeted Downstream-Agnostic Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19446)
*Zhuxin Lei,Ziyuan Yang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对预训练编码器的有针对性下游无关攻击方法，使用"威胁图像"作为特征级锚点，生成示例特定的对抗扰动，在更严格的威胁模型下实现高效攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有下游无关攻击方法在宽松的威胁模型下操作，只要改变原始预测即算成功，无需特定目标。但在实际攻击场景中，需要更严格的威胁模型，要求攻击既要有针对性又要下游无关。由于下游任务未知且编码器不直接产生预测，实现有针对性攻击尤其困难。

Method: 1) 提出有针对性下游无关攻击框架，引入"威胁图像"作为攻击者预设的特定目标；2) 设计生成器为每个图像生成示例特定的对抗扰动，而非传统共享扰动方法；3) 迫使受害编码器为对抗示例和威胁图像输出相同的特征表示；4) 利用威胁图像作为特征级锚点，构建任务无关的桥梁来揭示编码器漏洞。

Result: 在10种自监督方法、3个基准数据集上进行广泛实验，证明了方法的有效性，揭示了预训练编码器的显著脆弱性。相比之前生成共享扰动的方法，示例特定范式实现了更高的攻击成功率和不可见性。

Conclusion: 提出了第一个有针对性下游无关攻击方法，通过威胁图像和示例特定扰动生成策略，在严格威胁模型下成功攻击预训练编码器，揭示了这类模型的深层次安全漏洞，为防御方法研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Recently, pre-trained encoders have gained widespread use due to their strong capability in representation extraction. However, they are vulnerable to downstream-agnostic attacks (DAAs). Existing DAA methods operate under a permissive threat model, where an attack is successful if the generated downstream-agnostic adversarial examples (DAEs) change the original prediction, without requiring a specific target. In this paper, we propose a Targeted DAA (TDAA) method under a stricter threat model requiring the attack to be both targeted and downstream-agnostic. Since the downstream task is unknown and encoders do not directly produce predictions, achieving a targeted attack is particularly challenging. To address this, we introduce a novel component termed the 'threat image', pre-selected by the attacker as the target. Specifically, a generator is designed to produce example-specific adversarial perturbations that compel the victim encoder to output identical features for both the DAEs and the threat image. Unlike previous DAA methods that generate a single shared perturbation for all samples, which often fails due to image diversity, our method adopts an example-specific paradigm. This generates tailored perturbations for each image to ensure a high attack success rate and invisibility. By leveraging the threat image as a feature-level anchor, our method builds a task-agnostic bridge to reveal the vulnerabilities of the victim encoder. Extensive experiments on 10 self-supervised methods across 3 benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach and reveal the pronounced vulnerability of pre-trained encoders. The code will be made publicly available after the review period.

</details>


### [34] [EventPrune: Cascaded Event-Assisted Token Pruning for Efficient First-Person Dynamic Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19506)
*Pengtao Ma,Ziliang Zhou,Ciyu Ruan,Haoyang Wang,Kaiyuan Li,Zihang Gong,Wenhua Ding,Chen Gao,Jingao Xu,Xinlei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ECP是一种无需训练的事件级联剪枝框架，利用事件相机的高频运动线索作为连续的运动先验指导视觉token选择，在减少80%视觉token的同时提高了第一人称空间推理性能，实现了1.89倍推理加速和52%的GFLOPs降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称动态空间推理需要模型跟踪连续运动和精确几何结构，但基于Transformer的Video-LLMs的二次注意力成本使得密集视觉token计算昂贵。现有的token剪枝范式主要依赖离散静态快照，无法保留推理所需的运动和几何线索。

Method: 提出事件级联剪枝(ECP)框架，包含三个阶段：1）事件触发因果采样：锚定运动信息关键帧；2）事件引导运动显著性过滤：抑制事件不活跃的视觉token；3）事件注意力排名融合：用运动显著动态校准空间注意力。这是首个利用事件相机高频运动线索作为连续事件引导运动先验的无需训练框架。

Result: 在减少80%视觉token的情况下，ECP优于全token基线（37.62% vs. 36.31%），实现1.89倍推理加速和52% GFLOPs降低。在ESR-Real（首个真实世界RGB-事件基准）上，ECP比全token基线提高了2.68个百分点的准确率。

Conclusion: ECP通过利用事件相机的连续运动先验有效解决了Video-LLMs中视觉token剪枝的挑战，在保持第一人称空间推理性能的同时显著降低了计算成本，为高效动态视觉理解提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: First-person dynamic spatial reasoning requires models to track continuous motion and precise geometric structure, but the quadratic attention cost of Transformer-based Video-LLMs makes dense visual tokens computationally expensive. Existing token pruning paradigms predominantly rely on discrete static snapshots, failing to preserve the motion and geometric cues essential for reasoning. We propose Event Cascade Pruning (ECP), to our knowledge the first training-free framework that leverages the high-frequency motion cues from event cameras as a continuous event-guided motion prior to guide token selection. ECP combines three stages: Event-Triggered Causal Sampling to anchor motion-informative keyframes, Event-guided Motion Saliency Filtering to suppress event-inactive visual tokens, and Event-Attention Ranking Fusion to calibrate spatial attention with motion-salient dynamics. With 80% visual token reduction, ECP outperforms the full-token baseline (37.62% vs. 36.31%) while achieving 1.89x inference speedup and 52% GFLOPs reduction. We further introduce ESR-Real, the first real-world RGB-event benchmark for first-person spatial reasoning, where ECP improves accuracy by 2.68 percentage points over full-token baselines.

</details>


### [35] [Return of Frustratingly Easy Unsupervised Video Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19510)
*Pengfei Wei,Yiqun Sun,Zhiqiang Xu,Yiping Ke,Lawrence B. Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MetaTrans：一种简洁有效的无监督视频域适应方法，通过时空分离处理和简单的学习目标实现高性能跨域动作识别


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督视频域适应（UVDA）是一个实际但尚未充分探索的问题。当前需要有效的跨域视频适应方法来解决源域和目标域之间的分布差异问题

Method: 提出MetaTrans方法，采用简洁的学习目标（仅包含两个基本损失项），通过巧妙的模型架构设计，利用时空静态减法模块分别处理跨域视频的空间和时间分歧

Result: 在各种跨域动作识别任务上的广泛实验评估显示，相比最先进的UVDA基线方法，MetaTrans实现了显著的绝对适应性能提升和相对性能增益

Conclusion: MetaTrans提出了一种简单但高效的无监督视频域适应方法，通过时空分离处理策略有效解决了跨域视频适应问题，在保持简洁性的同时实现了优异的性能

Abstract: Unsupervised video domain adaptation (UVDA) is a practical but under-explored problem. In this paper, we propose a frustratingly easy UVDA method, called MetaTrans. Specifically, MetaTrans adopts a concise learning objective that contains only two fundamental loss terms. Despite the simplicity of the learning objective, MetaTrans embodies an advanced UVDA idea, that is, handling the spatial and temporal divergence of cross-domain videos separately, through a subtle model architecture design. By implementing a temporal-static subtraction module, MetaTrans effectively removes spatial and temporal divergence. Extensive empirical evaluations, particularly on various cross-domain action recognition tasks, show substantial absolute adaptation performance enhancement and significantly superior relative performance gain compared with state-of-the-art UVDA baselines.

</details>


### [36] [Are Watermarked Images Editable? SafeMark for Watermark-Preserving Text-Guided Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19511)
*Xiaodong Wu,Qi Li,Xiangman Li,Zelin Zhang,Lingshuang Liu,Jianbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SafeMark是一个水印保持框架，通过在水印解码损失上添加阈值约束来微调扩散编辑器，实现在文本引导图像编辑过程中不损害水印完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一个基本但未被充分研究的问题：水印图像能否在不损害水印完整性的情况下保持可编辑性。当前的图像编辑方法在保持水印完整性方面存在不足，这限制了在生成式编辑管道中实现可信图像来源追踪的能力。

Method: 提出SafeMark框架，将阈值水印解码损失直接添加到扩散编辑器的训练目标中，通过微调编辑器使得语义有效的编辑同时能够保持嵌入的水印。该方法具有信息论理论基础：保持编辑图像上的高比特精度下界了编辑器通道在水印和编辑输出之间保留的互信息。

Result: 在多数据集、多文本引导编辑方法和后编辑失真设置下的广泛评估表明，SafeMark在各种编辑设置下实现了高水印比特精度，同时保持了高质量的语义编辑，且没有牺牲对常见后编辑失真的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 语义可编辑性和水印完整性从根本上是可以兼容的，这使得在生成式编辑管道中实现可信的图像来源追踪成为可能。SafeMark无需架构修改，可与可微分的基于扩散的编辑器兼容。

Abstract: This paper investigates a fundamental yet underexplored question: can watermarked images remain editable without compromising watermark integrity? We propose SafeMark, a framework for watermark-preserving text-guided image manipulation that explicitly integrates watermark integrity into the editing process. Specifically, SafeMark adds a thresholded watermark-decoding loss directly to the diffusion editor's training objective, fine-tuning the editor so that semantically valid edits also preserve the embedded watermark at the final output. This design admits a clean information-theoretic justification: maintaining high bit-accuracy on the edited image lower-bounds the mutual information that the editor channel preserves between watermark and edited output, the quantity that fundamentally controls watermark recoverability. SafeMark is compatible with differentiable diffusion-based editors, and requires no architectural modification. Extensive evaluations across multiple datasets, text-guided editing methods, and post-edit distortion settings demonstrate that SafeMark achieves high watermark bit accuracy across diverse editing settings while maintaining high-quality semantic edits, without sacrificing robustness to common post-edit distortions. These results demonstrate that semantic editability and watermark integrity are fundamentally compatible, enabling trustworthy image provenance in generative editing pipelines.

</details>


### [37] [Dual-Prompt CLIP with Hybrid Visual Encoders for Occluded Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19527)
*Zhangjian Ji,Shaotong Qiao,Kai Feng,Wei Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DPL-ReID是一种用于遮挡行人重识别的新模型，结合了双提示学习策略、真实世界遮挡增强方法和加权门控特征融合机制，在多个基准数据集上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遮挡会破坏行人身体区域线索，使跨视角匹配复杂化。现有基于预训练视觉语言模型的行人重识别方法通常只关注增强提示学习，而忽略了遮挡物的语义信息。需要一种能够利用文本线索捕捉完整行人语义并保持遮挡鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 提出DPL-ReID模型，包含三个核心组件：1) 双提示学习策略(Dual-PL)，利用文本线索捕捉完整行人语义；2) 真实世界遮挡增强(RWOA)，真实模拟现实遮挡场景丰富遮挡样本；3) 加权门控特征融合(WGFF)，结合LSNet捕获全局信息并作为特征门控机制，指导CLIP视觉编码器生成更全面的特征表示。

Result: 在多个基准遮挡ReID数据集上的广泛实验表明，DPL-ReID取得了最先进的性能。遮挡实例库已在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: DPL-ReID通过创新的双提示学习、真实世界遮挡增强和加权门控特征融合，有效解决了遮挡行人重识别中的挑战，实现了优越的性能表现。

Abstract: Occluded person re-identification focuses on matching partially visible pedestrians across multiple camera views. However, occlusions disrupt body-region cues, thereby complicating cross-view matching. Most person ReID methods built on pretrained vision-language models only focus on enhancing prompt-based feature learning while ignoring the semantic information of occluders. Based on the success of CLIP-ReID, we propose a novel Dual Prompt Learning ReID (DPL-ReID) model for occluded person ReID. It incorporates a Dual Prompt Learning (Dual-PL) strategy, which can utilize textual cues to capture complete pedestrian semantics and keep robustness against occlusion, and a Real-World Occlusion Augmentation (RWOA) method that realistically simulates occlusion scenarios encountered in real word to enrich occluded samples. In addition, we also design a Weighted Gated Feature Fusion (WGFF) method, which in corporates LSNet to capture global information and act as a feature-gating mechanism. This mechanism can effectively guide the CLIP visual encoder toward generating more comprehensive feature representations. Extensive experiments on several benchmark occluded ReID datasets show that our proposed DPL-ReID achieves the state-of-the art performance. The occlusion instance library are available at https://github.com/stone-qiao/DPL-ReID.

</details>


### [38] [Towards Camera-Robust 3D Localization: Equation-Anchored Tool-Use for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19528)
*Xueying Jiang,Wenhao Li,Quanhao Qian,Deli Zhao,Shijian Lu,Gongjie Zhang,Ran Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个方程锚定的工具使用框架，通过将空间工具重新定义为公式变量，明确在思维链中编写针孔相机反投影方程，从而解决MLLMs中的3D定位问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型中的3D定位（包括3D目标检测和3D视觉定位）受到相机内参模糊性的根本限制：相同图像在不同相机参数下对应不同的3D场景。现有方法要么忽略相机参数并过拟合到规范训练内参，要么从外部工具检索深度和3D线索但仅将其视为参考线索，都无法确定性传播相机信息到预测中

Method: 提出方程锚定的工具使用框架：1）主动检索相机内参和采样多点度量深度；2）在思维链中明确编写针孔反投影方程 $\hat{X} = (u_c - c_x)\bar{Z}/f_x$；3）将工具输出代入公式后回归最终的9自由度边界框

Result: 在相机内参从0.5倍到1.5倍重新缩放的3D目标检测和3D视觉定位任务上，该方法优于仅RGB和工具增强的基线方法，在相机参数偏离训练尺度最大的情况下获得显著增益

Conclusion: 通过方程锚定的工具使用框架，将相机参数确定性纳入3D定位流程，解决了MLLMs中的相机内参模糊问题，显著提升了模型在未知相机参数下的泛化性能

Abstract: 3D localization in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), including 3D object detection and 3D visual grounding, is fundamentally limited by camera intrinsic ambiguity: the same image admits different 3D scenes under different cameras. Existing MLLMs either ignore camera parameters and overfit to a canonical training intrinsic, or retrieve depth and 3D cues from external tools but treat the returned values as reference cues (numerical hints that the model is free to interpret implicitly), both preventing camera information from being deterministically propagated into the prediction. We propose an equation-anchored tool-use framework that re-purposes spatial tools as formula variables. The proposed framework proactively retrieves camera intrinsics and samples multi-point metric depths, writes the pinhole back-projection equation $\hat{X} = (u_c - c_x)\bar{Z}/f_x$ explicitly in Chain-of-Thought (CoT), and substitutes tool outputs into the formula before regressing the final 9-DoF bounding box. On both 3D object detection and 3D visual grounding tasks under rescaled camera intrinsics from $0.5\times$ to $1.5\times$, our method outperforms RGB-only and tool-augmented baselines, with significant gains where the camera deviates most from the training scale. Code and data will be released.

</details>


### [39] [Boosting Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Core Token Attention-Based Seed Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19532)
*Yunzhe Zhang,Hongfu Liu,Pengyu Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ABSS是一种无需训练的方法，通过分析去噪过程中对核心提示词的注意力动态来预测并选择最佳种子，从而提高生成图像的质量和提示对齐度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型对初始种子高度敏感，不同种子会导致图像质量和提示对齐度出现显著差异。现有方法需要大量计算资源或依赖于固定的接受阈值，缺乏轻量高效的种子选择方案。

Method: 提出基于注意力的种子选择（ABSS）方法：1）在去噪早期阶段分析对提示核心令牌的跨注意力动态；2）基于注意力模式对候选种子进行评分和排序；3）保留排名前k的种子进行完整生成，丢弃其余种子。该方法无需微调，不改变初始噪声，纯推理时操作。

Result: 在三个基准测试中，ABSS为Stable Diffusion变体带来了文本-图像对齐度和视觉质量的一致提升。该方法作为现有种子优化流程的轻量级预选择附加组件，能获得额外增益，并通过人类偏好和对齐度指标得到验证。

Conclusion: 注意力动态是预测扩散模型生成质量的有效指标。ABSS提供了一种训练免费、即插即用的高效种子选择方法，显著提升了生成图像的可靠性和质量，同时保持了计算轻量性。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can synthesize high-quality images, yet the outcome is notoriously sensitive to the random seed: different initial seeds often yield large variations in image quality and prompt-image alignment. We revisit this "seed effect" and show that attention dynamics over prompt core tokens, the content-bearing words, measured during the first few denoising steps, strongly predict final generation quality. Building on this observation, we introduce Attention-Based Seed Selection (ABSS), a training-free, plug-and-play method that ranks seeds for a given prompt by leveraging cross-attention to core tokens during the denoising process. ABSS requires no finetuning and does not alter the initial noise; it scores and ranks all candidate seeds, keeps only the top-k for full generation, and discards the rest, without relying on a fixed accept/reject threshold. Operating purely at inference time, ABSS can serve as a lightweight pre-selection add-on for existing seed-optimization pipelines, enabling additional gains. Across three benchmarks, extensive experiments show that ABSS enables consistent improvements in text-image alignment and visual quality for Stable Diffusion variants, as corroborated by human preference and alignment metrics.

</details>


### [40] [Trust It or Not: Evidential Uncertainty for Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction with Trust3R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19539)
*Zihao Zhu,Wenyuan Zhao,Nuo Chen,Chao Tian,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Trust3R：一种轻量级证据不确定性框架，用于前馈式3D重建，通过门控残差均值细化和NIW证据头提供概率可靠的逐点几何不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前馈式几何基础模型的置信度分数是启发式的，缺乏概率解释，且无法可靠地指示预测几何的哪些部分以及多大程度上可信。需要一种能提供概率基础不确定性估计的方法。

Method: 结合门控残差均值细化与正态-逆Wishart证据头，产生一个封闭形式的多变量学生t分布，用于逐点几何不确定性估计。该方法在前向传播中提供概率基础的点云不确定性估计。

Result: 在室内外基准测试中，Trust3R持续改善了风险覆盖和稀疏化性能，普遍提升了几何精度。在ScanNet++上实现AURC降低25%、AUSE降低41%，提供更强的下游几何管道不确定性感知加权信号。

Conclusion: Trust3R提供了一个轻量级且实用的不确定性框架，能够在前馈式3D重建中提供概率可靠的不确定性估计，显著优于现有不确定性感知基线方法。

Abstract: Geometric foundation models hold promise for unconstrained dense geometry prediction from uncalibrated images. However, in current feed-forward designs, their predicted confidence scores are heuristic, lack probabilistic interpretation, and often fail to indicate where and how much the predicted geometry can be trusted. To address this gap, we present Trust3R, a lightweight evidential uncertainty framework for feed-forward 3D reconstruction. Trust3R combines gated residual mean refinement with a Normal-Inverse-Wishart evidential head, yielding a closed-form multivariate Student-t distribution for per-point geometric uncertainty. This design provides probabilistically grounded pointmap uncertainty estimates while adding moderate inference overhead. We evaluate on diverse indoor and outdoor benchmarks and compare against MASt3R's built-in confidence map as well as common uncertainty-aware baselines spanning single-pass heteroscedastic regression and sampling-based methods such as MC dropout and deep ensembles. Experimental results show that Trust3R consistently improves risk-coverage and sparsification, and generally improves geometric accuracy. These gains are reflected in stronger uncertainty ranking across benchmarks, with 25% lower AURC and 41% lower AUSE on ScanNet++, providing a practical reliability signal for uncertainty-aware weighting in downstream geometry pipelines. The project page and code are available at https://trust3r-z.github.io/.

</details>


### [41] [EgoCoT-Bench: Benchmarking Grounded and Verifiable Operation-Centric Chain of Thought Reasoning for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19559)
*Yang Dai,Dian Jiao,Tianwei Lin,Wenqiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoCoT-Bench：一个用于以自我为中心视频理解的细粒度、可验证的操作推理基准，包含3,172个可验证QA对，旨在评估多模态大语言模型在细粒度手-物体交互推理中的表现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的以自我为中心视频基准存在局限性：1）缺乏基础理性评估；2）对细粒度操作中心推理支持有限；3）很少检查模型推理是否基于明确的时空证据；4）需要验证模型解释是否与答案一致

Method: 提出EgoCoT-Bench基准，包含3,172个可验证QA对，覆盖351个以自我为中心视频，分为4个任务组共12个子任务组（感知与回溯、预期、高级推理）。采用时空场景图指导的生成框架构建，并由人工标注员精炼以确保正确性、以自我为中心相关性和细粒度质量

Result: 实验结果显示：1）多模态模型在以自我为中心细粒度推理方面持续存在困难；2）许多模型产生的解释虽然答案正确，但证据与答案不一致；3）揭示了当前模型在基础可验证推理方面的局限性

Conclusion: EgoCoT-Bench可以作为以自我为中心视频理解中基础和可验证推理的有用测试平台，促进多模态大语言模型在细粒度手-物体交互、物体状态变化跟踪和操作过程推理方面的发展

Abstract: The rapid development of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) has led to growing interest in egocentric video understanding, specifically the ability for MLLMs to recognize fine-grained hand-object interactions, track object state changes over time, and reason about manipulative processes in dynamic environments from a first-person perspective. However, existing egocentric video benchmarks suffer from \textbf{limited grounded rationale evaluation}, offering limited support for fine-grained operation-centric reasoning and rarely examining whether model rationales are grounded in explicit spatio-temporal evidence. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{EgoCoT-Bench}, a fine-grained egocentric benchmark for grounded and verifiable operation-centric reasoning with explicit step-by-step rationale annotations. Overall, EgoCoT-Bench comprises 3,172 verifiable QA pairs over 351 egocentric videos separated into four task groups for a total of 12 sub-task groups, encompassing perception and retrospection, anticipation, and high-level reasoning. The benchmark is constructed through a spatio-temporal scene graphs (STSG) guided generation framework and is further refined by human annotators to ensure correctness, egocentric relevance and fine-grained quality. Experimental results show continuing difficulties with egocentric fine-grained reasoning and further reveal that many multimodal models produce explanations that are answer-correct, but have evidence that is inconsistent with the answer. We hope EgoCoT-Bench can serve as a useful testbed for grounded and verifiable reasoning in egocentric video understanding. Project page and supplementary materials are available at: https://dstardust.github.io/EgoCoT/.

</details>


### [42] [Lens Privacy Sealing: A New Benchmark and Method for Physical Privacy-Preserving Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19578)
*Mengyuan Liu,Ziyi Wang,Peiming Li,Junsong Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Lens Privacy Sealing(LPS)硬件方案，通过可调层压膜物理模糊摄像头镜头，实现传感器前隐私保护，配合MSPNet算法在隐私保护的同时提升动作识别精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于RGB摄像头的监控系统用于公共安全和医疗保健的动作识别，但存在严重的隐私问题。现有方法依赖后捕获算法，无法在数据采集过程中保护隐私。

Method: 提出LPS硬件解决方案，使用可调层压膜物理模糊镜头；提出MSPNet单阶段框架，包含帧间噪声抑制器(IFNS)和跨帧语义聚合器(CFSA)，通过对比语言-图像预训练增强语义提取；创建P$^3$AR数据集包含P$^3$AR-NTU(114K视频)和P$^3$AR-PKU子集。

Result: MSPNet将动作识别准确率相比基线方法提升近一倍，同时将身份识别抑制到低水平；LPS相比最先进的硬件方法实现更优的隐私-效用权衡，抵抗包括PSF反演和数据驱动恢复在内的重建攻击，在不同光学配置和挑战环境中具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LPS提供了一种低成本、物理不可逆的传感器前隐私保护方案，配合MSPNet算法在有效保护隐私的同时保持良好的动作识别性能，在监控应用中实现了理想的隐私-效用平衡。

Abstract: RGB camera-based surveillance systems enable human action recognition for public safety and healthcare, yet raise serious privacy concerns. Existing methods rely on post-capture algorithms, which fail to protect privacy during data acquisition. We propose Lens Privacy Sealing (LPS), a simple hardware solution that physically obscures camera lenses with adjustable laminating film, providing pre-sensor privacy protection at minimal cost. Unlike software methods or expensive engineered optics, LPS achieves strong privacy through stochastic multi-layer scattering that is physically irreversible. We introduce the P$^3$AR dataset for privacy-preserving action recognition, featuring both large-scale replay-captured (P$^3$AR-NTU, 114K videos) and real-world collected (P$^3$AR-PKU) subsets with privacy attribute annotations. To handle video degradation from LPS, we propose MSPNet, a single-stage framework incorporating Inter-Frame Noise Suppressor (IFNS) and Cross-Frame Semantic Aggregator (CFSA), enhanced by contrastive language-image pre-training for robust semantic extraction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MSPNet with IFNS and CFSA nearly doubles action recognition accuracy compared to baseline methods while suppressing identity recognition to low levels. Comprehensive validation shows LPS achieves a superior privacy-utility trade-off compared to state-of-the-art hardware methods, resists reconstruction attacks including PSF inversion and data-driven recovery, and generalizes robustly across optical configurations and challenging environments. Code is available at https://github.com/wangzy01/MSPNet.

</details>


### [43] [deadtrees.earth-aerial: A Multi-Resolution Aerial Image Dataset for Tree Cover and Mortality Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19605)
*Ayushi Sharma,Clemens Mosig,Lukas Drees,Salim Soltani,Janusch Vajna-Jehle,Aaron Sheppard,Belqis Ahmadi,Jonathan Schmid,Paul Neumeier,Nathan Jacobs,Jan Dirk Wegner,Teja Kattenborn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了两个用于树木覆盖和死亡率联合分割的全球尺度航拍影像数据集：DTE-aerial-train训练集和DTE-aerial-bench基准测试集，为气候变化下的森林监测提供关键数据支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球森林面临气候变化、火灾、病虫害等多重威胁，迫切需要可扩展的树木覆盖和死亡率监测方法。现有方法受限于缺乏全球代表性、标准化的联合分割数据集，阻碍了航拍影像在森林监测中的应用进展。

Method: 创建了两个机器学习就绪的开放数据集：1) DTE-aerial-train训练集包含38.5万个1024×1024像素图像块，分辨率2.5-20厘米，带有专家标注和审核的多类伪标签；2) DTE-aerial-bench基准测试集包含25个全球分布的航拍影像，共525个图像块，提供高质量专家标注。数据集涵盖热带、温带、寒带和旱地生物群落。

Result: 使用基准测试集评估，建立了强大的参考基线，在所有生物群落和尺度上改进了死亡率分割，在挑战性区域（如北方森林）获得显著提升：F1分数从0.40提高到0.58，相对改进约45%。

Conclusion: 这两个数据集首次实现了全球尺度下厘米级航拍影像的树木覆盖和死亡率联合分割，填补了现有数据空白。所有数据、模型和代码将公开发布，为全球森林监测研究提供重要资源。

Abstract: Forests worldwide are increasingly threatened by climate change and disturbances such as fire, pests, and pathogens, creating an urgent need for scalable monitoring of tree cover and tree mortality. Aerial imagery from drones and aircraft is a key data source for detailed and large-scale mapping of tree crowns and mortality. However, related progress is limited by the lack of globally representative, harmonized datasets for joint segmentation of tree cover and mortality. We introduce two novel, open, machine-learning-ready datasets to enable joint segmentation of tree cover and tree mortality from centimeter-scale aerial imagery for the first time at global scales. With DTE-aerial-train, we provide a training dataset comprising 385K image patches of size 1024x1024 pixels, with resolutions ranging from 2.5 to 20 cm. It includes multi-class expert-annotated and -audited pseudo-labels for tree cover and mortality. With DTE-aerial-bench, we provide a geographically balanced benchmark test set of 25 globally distributed orthoimages totaling 525 patches with high-quality expert annotations for both tree cover and mortality. Both the training and benchmark datasets span tropical, temperate, boreal, and dryland biomes and cover a wide range of forest structures and mortality patterns. Using the benchmark test set for evaluation, we establish strong reference baselines that improve mortality segmentation across all biomes and scales with significant gains in challenging regions, such as boreal forests, where the F1 score increases from 0.40 to 0.58 with around 45% relative improvement. All data, models, and code will be publicly released under permissive open-source licenses. An interactive visualization of the benchmark dataset is available at deadtrees.earth/releases/dte-aerial-bench.

</details>


### [44] [Spectral Integrated Gradients for Coarse-to-Fine Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19607)
*Soyeon Kim,Seongwoo Lim,Kyowoon Lee,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Spectral Integrated Gradients (SIG) 是一种基于奇异值分解的积分梯度改进方法，通过渐进激活奇异分量来生成更清晰、噪声更少的特征归因图


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统积分梯度方法存在路径选择问题，标准直线路径会同时引入所有输入特征，导致梯度噪声累积，影响归因质量

Method: 基于基线到输入差异的奇异值分解构建积分路径，按奇异值从大到小的顺序渐进激活奇异分量，实现从全局结构到细节特征的递进激活

Result: 在多个图像分类数据集上的评估表明，SIG相比现有基于路径的归因方法生成更清晰的归因图，减少噪声，并取得改进的量化性能

Conclusion: Spectral Integrated Gradients通过SVD路径有效解决了传统积分梯度的噪声问题，提供了一种更优的特征归因方法

Abstract: Integrated Gradients (IG) is a widely adopted feature attribution method that satisfies desirable axiomatic properties. However, the choice of integration path significantly affects the quality of attributions, and the standard straight-line path introduces all input features simultaneously, often accumulating noisy gradients along the way. To address this limitation, we propose Spectral Integrated Gradients, which constructs integration paths based on singular value decomposition (SVD) of the baseline-to-input difference. By progressively activating singular components from largest to smallest, SIG introduces global structure before fine-grained details, naturally following a coarse-to-fine progression. Through extensive evaluation across diverse image classification datasets, we demonstrate that SIG produces cleaner attribution maps with reduced noise and achieves improved quantitative performance compared to existing path-based attribution methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/leekwoon/sig/.

</details>


### [45] [White-Balance First, Adjust Later: Cross-Camera Color Constancy via Vision-Language Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19613)
*Shuwei Li,Lei Tan,Robby T. Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLM-CC是一种基于视觉语言模型反馈引导的迭代式白平衡框架，通过将色彩恒常性重新定义为迭代感知反馈问题，显著提升了跨相机泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的色彩恒常性模型容易过拟合到训练相机的色彩响应特性，导致在其他相机拍摄的图像上性能下降，跨相机泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出反馈引导框架VLM-CC：将色彩恒常性重新定义为迭代细化过程。使用当前估计对图像进行白平衡并转换为伪sRGB，通过轻量级LoRA调优的VLM评估校正图像，识别主导残留色偏并提供定性反馈，将反馈映射到残差光照方向（红、绿、蓝），更新光照估计直至收敛。

Result: VLM-CC在多个数据集上实现了跨相机色彩恒常性的最先进鲁棒性，通过用VLM引导的感知反馈替代直接RGB回归，显著提升了泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将色彩恒常性重新表述为迭代感知反馈问题，利用VLM评估而非直接RGB回归，是提高跨相机泛化性能的有效方法，为传统白平衡问题提供了新视角。

Abstract: Color constancy aims to keep object colors consistent under varying illumination. Cross-camera generalization in color constancy remains challenging because learning-based models often overfit to the color response characteristics of the training camera, resulting in degraded performance on images captured by other cameras. We propose VLM-CC, a feedback-guided framework that formulates color constancy as an iterative refinement process. Instead of directly estimating the illuminant from raw input, VLM-CC performs iterative correction driven by vision-language model (VLM)-based evaluation. At each iteration, the image is white-balanced using the current estimate and converted to pseudo-sRGB. A lightweight LoRA-tuned VLM then assesses the corrected image, identifying the dominant residual color cast and providing qualitative feedback. This feedback is mapped to a residual illumination direction (red, green, or blue) and used to update the illuminant estimate until convergence. Our key idea is to reframe color constancy as an iterative perceptual feedback problem, leveraging VLM evaluation instead of direct RGB regression. By replacing direct RGB estimation with VLM-guided perceptual feedback, VLM-CC achieves state-of-the-art robustness in cross-camera color constancy across multiple datasets. Code will be available at https://github.com/NothingIknow/VLM-CC.

</details>


### [46] [Bézier Degradation Modeling for LiDAR-based Human Motion Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19620)
*Xiaoqi An,Lin Zhao,Jun Li,Chen Gong,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BMLiCap：一种基于LiDAR的粗到细3D人体运动捕捉框架，使用时间可压缩的Bézier曲线建模运动，通过多尺度变换器和聚合器处理遮挡和噪声，在多个基准测试中实现最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LiDAR基3D人体运动捕捉方法在输入不稳定和严重遮挡情况下容易出现抖动或预测失败，需要更鲁棒的运动表示和重建方法来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出BMLiCap框架：1) 使用时间可压缩Bézier曲线建模运动，通过轨迹保留策略减少控制点获得连贯的运动表示；2) 设计渐进运动重建模块，包含Time-scale Motion Transformer（TMT）预测多时间尺度运动曲线，以及Multi-level Motion Aggregator（MMA）自适应融合多尺度曲线恢复详细姿态；3) 有效桥接由遮挡和噪声造成的观测间隙。

Result: 在四个主流基准测试（LiDARHuman26M, FreeMotion, NoiseMotion, SLOPER4D）上实现最先进的精度和时间连续性，在复杂场景中能够补偿严重遮挡并减少预测抖动。

Conclusion: BMLiCap通过Bézier曲线表示和多尺度运动重建，有效解决了LiDAR基运动捕捉中的不稳定输入和遮挡问题，展示了在复杂场景中保持高精度和时间连贯性的能力。

Abstract: LiDAR-based 3D human motion capture has broad applications in fields such as autonomous driving and robotics, where accurate motion reconstruction is crucial. However, existing methods often struggle with unstable inputs and severe occlusions, leading to jittery or even failed pose predictions. To address these challenges, we propose BMLiCap, a coarse-to-fine framework that models motion using temporally compressible Bézier curves. By reducing control points through a trajectory-preserving strategy, we obtain a coherent and learning-friendly motion representation. To reconstruct human actions from LiDAR point-cloud cues, we design a progressive motion-reconstruction module. Specifically, a Time-scale Motion Transformer (TMT) is introduced to predict motion curves at multiple temporal scales, and a Multi-level Motion Aggregator (MMA) is utilized to adaptively fuse the multi-scale curves to recover detailed, temporally coherent poses, effectively bridging observation gaps caused by occlusions and noise. Across four mainstream benchmarks LiDARHuman26M, FreeMotion, NoiseMotion, and SLOPER4D, BMLiCap achieves state-of-the-art accuracy and temporal continuity in complex scenes, demonstrating its ability to compensate for severe occlusions and reduce prediction jitter.

</details>


### [47] [UniRefiner: Teaching Pre-trained ViTs to Self-Dispose Dross via Contrastive Register](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19622)
*Congpei Qiu,Zhaoyu Hu,Wei Ke,Zhuotao Tian,Yanhao Wu,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniRefiner是一种通用的ViT细化框架，能够通过对比寄存器识别并处理三种类型的伪令牌，只需少量数据微调即可显著提升大规模ViT模型在密集预测任务中的空间表示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Vision Transformer在空间敏感任务中存在伪令牌问题，这些伪令牌会影响空间表示。先前研究将伪令牌狭义地定义为高范数异常值，这种定义不足以解决密集预测任务中的实际问题，因为任何未编码位置对齐语义的令牌都应被视为伪令牌。

Method: 作者提出了UniRefiner框架，包含三个关键部分：1）系统性分类和表征三种基本类型的伪令牌；2）使用对比寄存器明确隔离和重新分配伪令牌；3）通过双重目标训练：将图像令牌与过滤后的常规令牌对齐以保留语义，将寄存器令牌与检测到的伪令牌对齐以捕获伪信号。

Result: UniRefiner只需在约5000张图像上进行少量迭代微调，即可显著提升多种ViT模型性能。例如，细化的EVA-CLIP-8B在ADE20K上达到51.9% mIoU（提升9.4%），超过DINOv2（49.1%），零样本分割准确率提升高达22%。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种更全面的伪令牌定义和分类方法，并通过UniRefiner框架解决了大规模ViT模型在空间表示中的伪令牌问题，释放了现有基础模型的空间潜力，为其在更广泛领域的应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Representation learning with Vision Transformers (ViTs) has advanced rapidly, yet the utility of large-scale models in spatially sensitive tasks is hindered by spurious tokens. Prior efforts to mitigate this have been limited, often defining these artifacts narrowly, for example, as simple high-norm outliers. We argue that this scope is insufficient. For dense prediction tasks, we posit that any token failing to encode location-aligned semantics should be treated as a spurious artifact. This broader definition reveals a more complex problem, leading us to systematically categorize and characterize three fundamental types of spurious tokens that corrupt spatial representations. Based on this comprehensive diagnosis, we propose UniRefiner, a universal refinement framework that teaches pre-trained ViTs to self-dispose of these artifacts. UniRefiner uses contrastive registers to explicitly isolate and redistribute spurious tokens via a dual objective: (i) it aligns image tokens with filtered regular tokens to preserve semantics, and (ii) it aligns register tokens with detected spurious tokens to capture the spurious signals. Our method requires only a few epochs of fine-tuning on ~5k images to refine diverse ViTs, including massive models like EVA-CLIP-8B and InternViT-6B. Experiments demonstrate consistent and significant improvements: notably, the refined EVA-CLIP-8B achieves 51.9\% mIoU on ADE20K (+9.4\%), surpassing specialized vision models like DINOv2 (49.1\%), while zero-shot segmentation accuracy improves by up to 22\%. UniRefiner unlocks the latent spatial potential of existing large-scale foundation models, paving the way for their broader application.

</details>


### [48] [PrAda: Few-Shot Visual Adaptation for Text-Prompted Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19623)
*Gabriele Rosi,Fabio Cermelli,Carlo Masone,Barbara Caputo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出原型适配方法PrAda，用于few-shot视觉适应，解决文本提示分割模型在专业领域性能下降的问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型虽能通过文本提示进行零样本分割，但在远离预训练的专业目标领域上性能显著下降。研究发现主要错误来源是分类而非掩模生成，因此需要适应新领域

Method: 提出原型适配方法PrAda，学习特定类别的原型，结合细粒度像素特征和高级transformer表示，然后通过学习的权重因子与原始基于文本的预测融合

Result: 在五个基准测试上的语义、实例和全景分割实验表明，PrAda相比最先进方法和基准线均有显著改进

Conclusion: PrAda是一种参数高效的适应方法，能在保持模型零样本潜力的同时实现对新领域的强适应，解决了文本提示分割模型在领域转移中的性能下降问题

Abstract: Segmenting images is critical for visual understanding but demands extensive pixel-level annotations. Foundational models have enabled new paradigms for predicting new classes guided by textual prompts, without annotations from the target domain. Yet, on specialized target domains, far from the original pre-training, their performance degrades. We study the errors of existing methods under such domain-shift, finding that misclassification rather than mask generation is the main culprit. To address this, we introduce the novel problem of Few-Shot Visual Adaptation for text-prompted Segmentation. This kind of adaptation has been largely studied for image classification, but it remains unexplored for segmentation. We tackle this task with Prototype Adaptation (PrAda), a novel, parameter-efficient method that adapts a frozen text-prompted segmentation model. Our approach learns class-specific prototypes by combining fine-grained pixel features and high-level transformer representations, which are then fused with the original text-based predictions through a learned importance factor. This preserves the model's zero-shot potential while enabling strong adaptation to new domains. Experiments across semantic, instance, and panoptic segmentation on five benchmarks demonstrate that PrAda yields significant improvements over state-of-the-art and proposed baselines.

</details>


### [49] [Component-Aware Structure-Preserving Style Transfer for Satellite Sim2Real 6D Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19624)
*Yonglong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一个组件感知的结构保持风格转换框架，用于卫星合成到真实数据构建，通过掩码对齐的调制将真实图像中的组件级风格代码注入到合成卫星区域，从而改进非合作卫星的单目6D姿态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非合作卫星的6D姿态估计严重依赖标注训练数据，但具有可靠姿态标签和组件级掩码的真实卫星图像难以大规模获取。合成渲染能提供精确几何标注，但渲染与真实观测之间的外观差距限制了直接向真实域的转移。

Method: 构建弱配对真实-合成样本，从校准真实采集、ArUco-based相机姿态测量、CAD渲染和组件掩码中提取。提取部分真实域风格代码并将其通过掩码对齐调制注入到相应的合成卫星区域。结合对抗训练、局部对比一致性、自正则化和边缘保持约束来保持生成图像可用于下游监督。

Result: 在5,000张渲染卫星图像和100张校准设置下捕获的真实图像上进行实验。相比代表性图像转换基线，该方法实现了最低的图像分布差异（FID为54.32，KID为0.048）。使用转换数据训练GDRNet时，ADD通过率提高到0.260，AUC提高到0.611。

Conclusion: 组件级外观转换可以在考虑校准设置的同时改进卫星Sim2Real姿态估计，同时保留模拟衍生的几何标注。这表明针对部件级别的外观迁移在卫星合成到真实的姿态估计任务中是有效的。

Abstract: Monocular 6D pose estimation for non-cooperative satellites depends heavily on annotated training data, yet real satellite images with reliable pose labels and component-level masks are difficult to acquire at scale. Synthetic rendering can provide exact geometric annotations, but the appearance gap between rendered and real observations limits direct transfer to the real domain. This paper presents a component-aware structure-preserving style transfer framework for satellite synthetic-to-real data construction. The method builds weakly paired real--synthetic samples from calibrated real acquisition, ArUco-based camera-pose measurement, CAD rendering, and component masks. It then extracts part-wise real-domain style codes from unlabeled real images and injects them into corresponding synthetic satellite regions through mask-aligned modulation. To keep the generated images usable for downstream supervision, adversarial training is combined with local contrastive consistency, self-regularization, and edge-preserving constraints. Experiments are conducted on 5,000 rendered satellite images and 100 real images captured in a calibrated setup. The real images provide target-domain appearance references and final evaluation images, while the downstream GDRNet pose estimator is trained only on synthetic or translated synthetic images. Compared with representative image-translation baselines, the proposed method achieves the lowest image distribution discrepancy, with an FID of 54.32 and a KID of 0.048. When the translated data are used to train GDRNet in this target-domain adaptation setting, the ADD pass rate improves to 0.260 and the AUC improves to 0.611. These results indicate that component-level appearance transfer can improve satellite Sim2Real pose estimation in the considered calibrated setup while retaining simulation-derived geometric annotations.

</details>


### [50] [P2DNav: Panorama-to-Downview Reasoning for Zero-shot Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19634)
*Kai Sheng,Liuyi Wang,Haojie Dai,Jinlong Li,Yongrui Qin,Zongtao He,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: P2DNav是一个用于零样本视觉语言导航的分层框架，通过分解全景方向选择和局部地面预测两个阶段，提高了导航决策的稳定性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本方法通常依赖额外的路径点预测模块，这些模块将高级方向推理与细粒度局部地面预测纠缠在一起，导致决策容易出错且不稳定。需要一种能够明确分解这两个过程的框架来解决这个问题。

Method: P2DNav包含三个核心组件：全景到俯视图转换（P2D）、滑动窗口对话记忆（SDM）和反射重定向机制（RRM）。P2D将导航决策分为两个阶段：全景方向选择和俯视图局部地面预测。SDM将导航历史组织为多轮对话上下文，并通过滑动窗口维护最近的视觉观察以支持长距离导航。RRM通过评估基于俯视图观察的局部地面可靠性，并在必要时返回全景方向选择来实现反射重定向。

Result: 在R2R-CE基准测试中，P2DNav在零样本方法中表现出色。与最先进的基于路径点和无路径点零样本方法相比，分别实现了146.6%和58.9%的成功率提升，证明了P2D、SDM和RRM在零样本VLN中的有效性。

Conclusion: P2DNav通过明确分解导航决策过程，提供了更稳定和准确的零样本视觉语言导航解决方案。该分层框架有效解决了现有方法中方向推理与局部地面预测纠缠的问题，显著提高了导航性能。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) requires an embodied agent to ground natural-language instructions into executable navigation actions in unseen environments. Existing zero-shot methods typically rely on additional waypoint prediction modules, which often entangle high-level directional reasoning with fine-grained local grounding, leading to error-prone and unstable decisions. In this paper, we propose P2DNav, a hierarchical framework for zero-shot vision-and-language navigation. P2DNav consists of three core components: Panorama-to-Downview (P2D), Sliding-Window Dialogue Memory (SDM), and Reflective Reorientation Mechanism (RRM). P2D explicitly decomposes navigation decision-making into two stages: panoramic direction selection and downview local grounding. It first selects the instruction-relevant direction from a 360° panorama, and then predicts a pixel-level target point from the downview RGB observation in that direction. In addition, SDM organizes navigation history as a multi-turn dialogue context and maintains recent visual observations within a sliding window to support long-horizon navigation. RRM further enables reflective reorientation by assessing the reliability of local grounding based on the downview observation and returning to panoramic direction selection when necessary. Experiments on the R2R-CE benchmark show that P2DNav achieves strong performance among zero-shot methods. In particular, compared with the state-of-the-art (SOTA) zero-shot waypoint-based and waypoint-free methods, P2DNav achieves SR gains of 146.6% and 58.9%, respectively, demonstrating the effectiveness of P2D, SDM, and RRM for zero-shot VLN. Code will be released for public use.

</details>


### [51] [Benchmarking and Evolving Reason-Reflect-Rectify for Reflective Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19639)
*Junjie Wang,Xinghua Lou,Jason Li,Ye Tian,Keyu Chen,Yulin Li,Bin Kang,Jacky Mai,Yanwei Li,Zhuotao Tian,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: R^3-Refiner：一个用于反射式视觉生成的双阶段框架，通过R^3循环（推理-反思-修正）和强化学习优化，提升文本到图像模型处理复杂提示的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到图像模型和统一多模态模型采用单次生成范式，难以处理需要迭代优化的复杂提示。模型能够识别生成错误，但缺乏生成可操作的修正指令的能力。

Method: 1. 提出R^3循环（Reason-Reflect-Rectify）框架作为反射式视觉生成的核心；2. 构建R^3-Bench基准（600多个专家标注实例）量化迭代推理和修正能力；3. 提出R^3-Refiner双阶段框架，采用组相对策略优化和分层奖励机制，将反思与修正对齐。

Result: R^3-Refiner在R^3-Bench上取得显著提升（反射判断分数+12.0%，修正分数+9.0%）；可无缝集成多种大语言模型，提升不同文本到图像模型在GenEval++和T2I-CompBench上的生成质量。

Conclusion: R^3框架为反射式视觉生成提供系统化解决方案，通过迭代的推理-反思-修正循环和强化学习优化，显著提升模型处理复杂生成任务的能力，填补了现有模型识别错误但无法有效修正的关键空白。

Abstract: Text-to-Image (T2I) models and Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) have achieved remarkable progress in visual generation. However, their reliance on a single-pass generation paradigm limits their ability to handle complex prompts requiring iterative refinement. To enable multi-round Reflective Visual Generation (RVG), we formalize the Reason-Reflect-Rectify (R^3) loop as a core framework and introduce R^3-Bench, a benchmark of over 600 expert-annotated instances that quantifies iterative reasoning and rectification capabilities. Evaluation on R^3-Bench reveals a critical gap: while state-of-the-art models can identify generation errors, they fail to generate actionable rectification instructions. To bridge this gap, we propose R^3-Refiner, a dual-stage framework leveraging Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and a Hierarchical Reward Mechanism (HRM) to better align rectification with reflective reasoning. Experiments show that R^3-Refiner achieves significant improvements on R^3-Bench (+12.0% in Reflective Verdict Score, +9.0% in Rectification Score), and can be seamlessly integrated with various MLLMs to enhance the generation quality of different T2I models on GenEval++ and T2I-CompBench. Code is available at https://github.com/xiaomoguhz/R3-Bench.

</details>


### [52] [Cross-View Splatter: Feed-Forward View Synthesis with Georeferenced Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19656)
*Matias Turkulainen,Akshay Krishnan,Filippo Aleotti,Mohamed Sayed,Guillermo Garcia-Hernando,Juho Kannala,Arno Solin,Gabriel Brostow,Daniyar Turmukhambetov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Cross-View Splatter是一种前馈方法，通过融合卫星鸟瞰视图和地面GPS标记照片，在统一3D坐标框架中预测像素对齐的高斯分布，用于室外场景重建和新型视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室外场景的忠实重建需要良好的相机覆盖，但地面图像采集耗时且难以大规模获取。卫星图像可通过公共API轻松访问，提供全局几何先验信息，有助于改善场景覆盖范围。

Method: 通过将正射校正的卫星视图与GPS标记的地面照片融合，在统一3D坐标框架中预测像素对齐的高斯分布。通过对齐地面和鸟瞰视角的特征表示，将地面特征提升到三维空间。

Result: 与仅使用地面图像的方法相比，该方法提高了场景覆盖范围和新型视角合成质量。在包含地理参考图像的新型视图合成基准测试中评估，并与现有最先进方法进行比较。

Conclusion: Cross-View Splatter通过融合卫星和地面多视角数据，为大规模室外场景重建提供了一种有效的解决方案，代码和数据准备将开源。

Abstract: We present Cross-View Splatter, a feed-forward method that predicts pixel-aligned Gaussian splats for outdoor scenes captured at ground level AND by satellite. Faithful reconstructions require good camera coverage, but ground imagery is time-consuming and hard to capture at scale for large outdoor scenes. Fortunately, satellite imagery can provide a global geometric prior that is easy to access via public APIs. Cross-View Splatter fuses orthorectified satellite views with GPS-tagged ground photos to predict Gaussian splats in a unified 3D coordinate frame. By aligning ground and bird's-eye feature representations, our model improves scene coverage and novel-view synthesis, compared to ground imagery alone. We train on curated georeferenced datasets and paired satellite-terrain data, mined from open mapping services. We evaluate our method on a new benchmark for novel-view synthesis with georeferenced imagery allowing comparison to prior state-of-the-art methods. Our code and data preparation will be available at https://nianticspatial.github.io/cross-view-splatter/.

</details>


### [53] [WBCAtt+: Fine-Grained Pixel-Level Morphological Annotations for White Blood Cell Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19692)
*Satoshi Tsutsui,Winnie Pang,Shuting He,Bihan Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WBCAtt+是一个新颖的白细胞图像数据集，包含11个形态属性和5个像素级细胞组分的密集标注，提供了113k图像级标签和10k分割图，用于支持可解释的血液病诊断研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有白细胞数据集主要标注细胞类别，缺乏病理学家用于解释细胞判断所需的详细形态学特征标注，这限制了可解释AI模型在血液病诊断中的发展。

Method: 1. 创建WBCAtt+数据集，包含11个形态学属性和5个像素级细胞组分的密集标注；2. 提供属性识别和语义分割的基线模型；3. 设计考虑细胞组成结构的属性识别模型以提升性能。

Result: WBCAtt+是首个提供全面标注的白细胞图像数据集，包含113k图像级标签和10k分割图。基于此数据集开发的模型在属性识别任务上表现出改进性能，并支持各种应用如可解释AI模型和反事实示例生成。

Conclusion: WBCAtt+数据集填补了白细胞图像研究中详细形态学标注的空白，为开发可解释的血液病诊断AI模型提供了重要资源，公开的数据集和代码将促进相关领域的研究进展。

Abstract: The microscopic examination of white blood cells (WBCs) plays a fundamental role in pathology and is essential for diagnosing blood disorders such as leukemia and anemia. To support further research on WBC images, multiple datasets have been proposed. However, they mainly annotate cell categories, and lack detailed morphological characteristics that pathologists use to explain their interpretations of cells. To address this gap, we introduce WBCAtt+, a novel dataset of WBC images densely annotated with 11 morphological attributes and five pixel-level cell components. With 113k image-level labels and 10k segmentation maps, WBCAtt+ is the first to provide comprehensive annotations for WBC images. Leveraging this dataset, we provide baseline models for attribute recognition and semantic segmentation. We also design an attribute recognition model to incorporate compositional structure of cells, further improving the recognition performance. Lastly, we showcase various applications enabled by our dataset, such as explainable AI models, including counterfactual example generation. \revision{The dataset and code are publicly available\footnote{https://doi.org/10.57967/hf/8143}}.

</details>


### [54] [FineBench: Benchmarking and Enhancing Vision-Language Models for Fine-grained Human Activity Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19846)
*Gueter Josmy Faure,Min-Hung Chen,Jia-Fong Yeh,Hung-Ting Su,Winston H. Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FineBench是一个细粒度人中心视频问答基准测试，包含19.9万问答对，覆盖64个长视频，用于评估VLMs在人类动作和交互的细微理解能力；FineAgent是一个模块化框架，通过定位器和描述器增强VLMs性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在细粒度视频理解方面存在不足，特别是在需要细微解释人类动作和交互的实际应用中。虽然近期有一些人中心基准测试评估模型行为，但它们未能同时结合长视频、密集问答覆盖和帧级时空定位，因此需要一个新的基准测试来填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 提出FineBench基准：包含64个15分钟长视频，199,420个多项选择题问答对，密集注释关注人物移动、人物交互和物体操作；2) 提出FineAgent框架：采用模块化设计，包含定位器和描述器组件，增强VLMs对视频的细粒度理解能力。

Result: 评估显示专有模型表现尚可，但开源VLMs表现显著不佳，尤其在多人场景的空间推理和区分人类动作/交互的细微差异方面存在困难。FineAgent框架实验表明能持续改进各种开源VLMs在FineBench上的性能。

Conclusion: FineBench为细粒度人中心视频理解研究提供了严谨的测试平台，而FineAgent为增强当前VLMs的此类推理能力提供了实用方法。这项工作填补了现有基准测试的空白，推动了视频理解向更细微层面发展。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in general video understanding, yet they often struggle with the fine-grained comprehension crucial for real-world applications requiring nuanced interpretation of human actions and interactions. While some recent human-centric benchmarks evaluate aspects of model behaviour such as fairness/ethics, emotion perception, and broader human-centric metrics, they do not combine long-form videos, very dense QA coverage, and frame-level spatial/temporal grounding at scale. To bridge this gap, we introduce FineBench, a human-centric video question answering (VQA) benchmark specifically designed to assess fine-grained understanding. FineBench comprises 199,420 multiple-choice QA pairs densely annotated across 64 long-form videos (15 minutes each), focusing on detailed person movement, person interaction, and object manipulation, including compositional actions. Our extensive evaluation reveals that while proprietary models like GPT-5 achieve respectable performance, current open-source VLMs significantly underperform, struggling particularly with spatial reasoning in multi-person scenes and distinguishing subtle differences in human movements and interactions. To address these identified weaknesses, we propose FineAgent, a modular framework that enhances VLMs by leveraging a Localizer and a Descriptor. Experiments show that FineAgent consistently improves the performance of various open VLMs on FineBench. FineBench provides a rigorous testbed for future research into fine-grained human-centric video understanding, while FineAgent offers a practical approach to enhance such reasoning in current VLMs.

</details>


### [55] [Physics-informed simulation framework for realistic sonar image generation and statistical validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19712)
*Kamal Basha S,Athira Nambiar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ACOUSIM是一个基于物理的声纳模拟与验证平台，通过控制海底纹理、光照阴影、平台高度和噪声等参数生成合成声纳图像，并使用统计方法定量评估合成与真实声纳图像的分布对齐度，为水下图分析提供可靠的模拟到真实验证基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成声纳数据集相比真实采集具有成本效益，但缺乏严格的定量验证限制了其实际应用价值，需要建立可量化的模拟到真实评估框架来验证合成数据的可靠性。

Method: 使用基于Gazebo的环境生成声纳图像，显式控制海底纹理、光照驱动的阴影、平台高度和噪声参数。通过全局强度分布和局部纹理（LBP）分布，使用Kullback-Leibler散度、Jensen-Shannon散度和Earth Mover's Distance等统计指标，在两个公开声纳数据集（SeabedObjects-KLSG-II和Sonar Common Target Detection）上量化合成图像与真实图像的相似度。

Result: 所有类别都显示出强纹理对齐（KL < 0.07）。平面类别的强度对齐优于船舶类别，这归因于阴影几何形状的复杂性差异。ACOUSIM成功建立了可重复的分布级基准，支持水下图像分析的可靠数据集验证。

Conclusion: ACOUSIM提供了一个基于物理的框架，用于定量评估合成声纳图像的真实性，填补了现有合成声纳数据集缺乏统计验证的空白。该平台为模拟到真实的声纳评估建立了可重复的分布级基准，直接支持水下图像分析任务的数据集验证需求。

Abstract: Synthetic sonar datasets offer a scalable alternative to costly real-world acquisition, yet their utility remains limited by the absence of rigorous quantitative validation. We present ACOUSIM (ACOustic SIMulation and Validation Platform), a physics-informed framework that evaluates the statistical alignment between synthetic and real sonar imagery without relying on generative models. A Gazebo-based environment generates sonar-like images by explicitly controlling seabed texture, illumination-driven shadowing, platform altitude, and noise. Realism is quantified against two public sonar datasets, SeabedObjects-KLSG-II and Sonar Common Target Detection (SCTD), using global intensity and local texture (LBP) distributions assessed via Kullback-Leibler divergence, Jensen-Shannon divergence, and Earth Mover's Distance. Results show strong texture alignment (KL < 0.07) across all classes, with plane-class intensity alignment outperforming ship-class due to shadow geometry complexity. ACOUSIM establishes a reproducible, distribution-level baseline for sim-to-real sonar evaluation and directly supports reliable dataset validation for underwater image analysis.

</details>


### [56] [Physics-in-the-Loop: A Hybrid Agentic Architecture for Validated CAD Engineering Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19717)
*Elias Berger,Muhammad Usama,Jan Mehlstäubl,Bernhard Saske,Kristin Paetzold-Byhain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Hybrid Agentic-Physical Architecture，将知识工程工具嵌入AI智能体决策循环，通过物理验证指导CAD设计的迭代生成，在结构复杂性和编译成功率上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）能够生成计算机辅助设计（CAD），但缺乏物理理解能力，无法保证工程设计的可靠性。传统方法试图从数据中隐式学习物理规律存在局限性。

Method: 提出混合智能体-物理架构，将经验证的知识工程工具直接嵌入自主AI智能体的决策循环。将工程设计建模为闭环顺序决策过程，由专用智能体基于载荷工况，使用知识工程工具作为反馈信号，迭代进行规划、生成、评估和修改。

Result: 系统生成更复杂且经过物理验证的设计：结构复杂度提高4.2倍，编译成功率相比类似智能体方法提高3.5%。提供了基准数据集和评估指标，代码库、提示和数据集将公开以支持复现性研究。

Conclusion: 通过将知识工程工具显式嵌入AI智能体决策循环，而不是依赖隐式学习物理规律，能够生成更可靠、经过物理验证的工程设计，为生成式CAD提供了功能性验证的框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate Computer-Aided Design (CAD), yet lack physical comprehension required for reliable engineering design. Instead of attempting to implicitly learn physical laws from data, we propose a Hybrid Agentic-Physical Architecture that embeds validated knowledge-based engineering tools directly into the decision making loop of autonomous AI agents. In this framework, engineering design is formulated as a closed-loop, sequential decision making process guided by explicit physical verification. Based on a load case, dedicated agents iteratively plan, generate, evaluate, and revise engineering designs using knowledge-based tools as a feedback signal. We introduce a benchmark dataset and metrics for assessing functional validity in generative CAD. Our system generates more complex and physically verified designs, with a 4.2 increase in structural complexity and improving compile rate by 3.5% compared to similar agentic methods. The codebase, prompts and dataset will be made publicly available to support reproducibility and future research.

</details>


### [57] [Rethinking Visual Attribution for Chest X-ray Reasoning in Large Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20158)
*Guangzhi Xiong,Qiao Jin,Sanchit Sinha,Zhiyong Lu,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MedFocus，一种基于概念的视觉归因方法，用于评估大型视觉语言模型在医学影像（胸部X光）中的可信度，发现现有归因方法往往无法准确识别模型决策所使用的证据，而MedFocus通过不平衡最优传输和针对性干预显著优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在医疗应用中表现出潜力，但其响应缺乏对视觉证据的忠实基础，引发了临床可信度的严重担忧。现有视觉归因方法用于解释模型预测，但这些解释是否真实反映模型决策所依据的视觉证据大多未经验证，因为通常缺乏模型内部推理的真实标注。

Method: 研究者开发了一个因果评估框架：1）只保留经过专家标注区域验证的CXR-VQA样本；2）通过反事实编辑验证该区域对模型预测的因果责任；3）在11种归因方法、6个开源LVLMs和两种输出模式上进行评估。为解决现有方法的不足，提出了MedFocus方法：通过不平衡最优运输定位具有临床意义的解剖区域，并通过针对性干预测量这些区域对模型输出的因果效应。

Result: 研究发现现有归因方法常常无法识别LVLMs使用的证据。MedFocus方法在空间、概念和标记级别产生归因，显著优于先前方法。该方法提供了空间级、概念级和标记级的归因。

Conclusion: MedFocus方法在医学大型视觉语言模型的归因方面取得了显著改进，朝着更可信的医疗AI解释迈出了一步。该研究强调了评估模型解释可信度的重要性，并为医学影像分析提供了更可靠的归因工具。

Abstract: Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) show promise in medical applications, but their inability to faithfully ground responses in visual evidence raises serious concerns about clinical trustworthiness. While visual attribution methods are widely used to explain LVLM predictions, whether these explanations actually reflect the visual evidence underlying the model's decision is largely unverified, since ground-truth annotations for internal model reasoning are typically unavailable. We address this question for chest X-ray (CXR) reasoning by developing a causal evaluation framework that retains only CXR-VQA samples for which the expert-annotated region is verified, via counterfactual editing, to be causally responsible for the model's prediction. Using this framework across 11 attribution methods, six open-source LVLMs, and two output modes (direct answer and step-by-step reasoning), we find that existing attribution methods often fail to identify the evidence used by LVLMs. To address this failure, we propose MedFocus, a concept-based attribution method that localizes clinically meaningful anatomical regions via unbalanced optimal transport and measures their causal effect on model outputs through targeted interventions. MedFocus produces spatial, concept-level, and token-level attributions and substantially outperforms prior methods, taking a step toward more trustworthy attribution for medical LVLMs. Our data and code are available at https://github.com/gzxiong/medfocus/.

</details>


### [58] [Efficient Long-Context Modeling in Diffusion Language Models via Block Approximate Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19726)
*Wenhu Zhang,Yiming Wu,Huanyu Wang,Yaoyang Liu,Huanzhang Dou,Senqiao Yang,Sitong Wu,Hanbin Zhao,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BA-Att（Block Approximate Sparse Attention）框架，通过块级预下采样操作在紧凑空间识别信息区域，避免依赖脆弱的先验采样模式，在保持接近全注意力性能的同时实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLMs）虽具有全局一致性和双向生成优势，但对超长序列处理成本高昂。现有块稀疏注意力方法采用固定采样模式（如尾部区域或反对角条纹），可能遗漏重要标记且在分布偏移下不稳定。

Method: 提出BA-Att框架：1) 块级预下采样操作在紧凑空间识别信息区域；2) 理论分析定义后下采样注意力映射并形式化预/后下采样方案间的近似误差；3) 引入轻量级范数排序模块和协方差补偿校正，使用对角线QK方差近似完整协方差以降低计算复杂度。

Result: 实验显示：1) 注意力计算比FlashAttention加速高达6.95倍；2) 在50%稀疏度下，在语言模型、多模态语言模型和视频生成模型中均保持接近全注意力性能；3) 展现出强效的效率和泛化能力。

Conclusion: BA-Att框架通过避免依赖位置先验的块选择策略，在紧凑下采样空间有效识别信息区域，结合理论分析和轻量补偿机制，实现了计算效率与性能的良好平衡，为长序列处理提供了一种有效的稀疏注意力解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) enable globally coherent, bidirectional, and controllable text generation, offering advantages over traditional autoregressive LLMs, while scaling to ultra-long sequences remains costly. Many existing block-sparse attention methods select blocks by fixed sampling patterns over the high-resolution attention space, such as tail regions or anti-diagonal stripes. Such prior-driven sampling can miss salient tokens and introduce instability under distribution shifts. In this paper, we propose the Block Approximate Sparse Attention framework (BA-Att) with block-wise pre-downsampled operation, which identifies informative regions within a compact downsampled space, avoiding reliance on brittle positional priors. To analyze its theoretical behavior, we define an oracle post-downsample attention map and formalize the approximation error between pre- and post-downsample schemes. Based on this insight, we introduce a lightweight norm-sorting module and a covariance-compensated correction that approximates full covariance using diagonal QK variances, reducing computational complexity. Extensive experiments show that our operator achieves up to 6.95x acceleration over FlashAttention in attention computation, and maintains near full-attention performance at 50% sparsity across language models, multimodal language models, and video generation models, demonstrating strong efficiency and generalization.

</details>


### [59] [GeoMamba: A Geometry-driven MambaVision Framework and Dataset for Fine-grained Optical-SAR Object Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19734)
*Tiantong Fang,Xiuwei Wang,Jing Xiao,Wujie Zhou,Liang Liao,Mi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoMamba是一个针对光学-SAR细粒度目标检索的几何驱动框架，通过几何特征注入模块和几何一致性约束模块解决模态差异问题，在新建的FGOS-as数据集上取得了63.3% mAP和77.0% Rank-1的优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源遥感提供了地面物体的互补观测，但光学与SAR图像在未对齐条件下的跨模态细粒度目标检索仍面临挑战。实际应用中存在显著的模态差异、散斑噪声和结构不一致性问题，限制了鲁棒的跨模态表示学习。

Method: 提出GeoMamba框架，包含几何特征注入模块（增强跨模态特征交互和融入结构先验）、几何一致性约束模块（使用经典算子施加分层几何约束）以及深度监督策略。这些组件共同促进几何一致的特征学习并保持目标结构信息。

Result: 构建了FGOS-as数据集（包含11个航空航天和海事类别），用于评估真实遥感场景下的未对齐跨模态细粒度目标检索。实验表明GeoMamba在所有对比方法中表现最优，在全对全检索设置下达到63.3% mAP和77.0% Rank-1准确率。

Conclusion: GeoMamba通过几何驱动的框架有效解决了光学-SAR跨模态细粒度检索的挑战，在保持目标结构信息的同时提升了检索性能，为多源遥感数据融合应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-source remote sensing enables complementary observation of ground objects, while cross-modal fine-grained object retrieval remains challenging, especially under unaligned optical and SAR conditions. Unlike conventional retrieval settings that rely on paired or spatially aligned samples, practical optical-SAR retrieval is affected by substantial modality discrepancy, speckle noise, and structural inconsistency, which limit robust cross-modal representation learning. To address this problem, we propose GeoMamba, a geometry-driven framework tailored for optical-SAR fine-grained retrieval. Specifically, GeoMamba introduces a Geometric Feature Injection (GFI) module that enhances cross-modal feature interaction and incorporates structural priors, thereby improving the robustness of SAR representations and promoting geometry-consistent feature learning. In addition, a Geometric Consistency Constraint (GCC) module, together with a Deep Supervision (DS) strategy, imposes hierarchical geometric constraints using classical operators, which helps preserve informative object structures during representation learning. We further construct a new dataset, FGOS-as, containing 11 aerospace and maritime categories for evaluating unaligned cross-modal fine-grained object retrieval in realistic remote sensing scenarios. Extensive experiments on FGOS-as demonstrate that GeoMamba outperforms existing methods, achieving 63.3% mAP and 77.0% Rank-1 accuracy in all-to-all retrieval setting.

</details>

Abstract: Recent advances in flow matching models have significantly improved text-to-image generation quality, but also introduce growing safety risks due to the generation of harmful or undesirable content. Existing concept erasure methods are either inference-time interventions with limited effectiveness or rely on supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which requires precisely aligned data and struggles with scalability and multi-concept settings. In this paper, we propose \emph{FlowErase-RL}, the first GRPO-based framework for concept erasure in flow matching models. We reformulate concept erasure as a reward optimization problem and introduce a \textbf{dynamic dual-path reward mechanism} that jointly optimizes (i) a Concept Erasure (CE) reward to suppress target concepts and (ii) a Non-target Space (NS) reward to preserve generative fidelity. The two reward paths are adaptively balanced during training via a performance-driven switching strategy, enabling stable optimization without explicit supervision. Extensive experiments on nudity, object, and artistic style erasure demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art erasure performance while maintaining strong image quality and semantic alignment. Moreover, it exhibits robust resistance to adversarial attacks and scales effectively to multi-concept scenarios. Our results establish a new paradigm for safe and controllable generation in flow matching models.

</details>

Abstract: Detecting anomalies in traffic scenes is crucial for ensuring safety in autonomous driving, yet collecting representative anomalous data remains challenging. Existing anomaly detection methods are highly specialized and rely on normality as defined by the abstract semantic Cityscapes classes, making it difficult to adapt to diverse real-world scenarios. We propose an adaptable real-time anomaly detection method that leverages foundation models in the form of pretrained vision transformer embeddings to detect deviations via nearest-neighbor similarity in the latent semantic feature space. Based on patch-wise processing, the algorithm produces dense anomaly masks, allowing for the localization of detected anomalies. The method robustly models normality through a single reference image. This formulation avoids explicit supervision and dataset-specific training, making it suitable for real-world deployment. We evaluate the method on standard benchmarks and on an automated vehicle in real-world scenarios. Despite its simplicity, the method achieves good performance on the Road Anomaly benchmark and demonstrates consistent qualitative behavior in practice, successfully highlighting semantically unusual objects in diverse scenes. These results suggest that simple, reference-based methods can provide useful anomaly signals under realistic operating conditions.
