Result: 检查清单改进提示获得最高平均分数（7.50/8），原始提示为5.67，澄清问题提示为6.67。检查清单提示还表现出最佳的质量-努力权衡，使用比原始提示和澄清提示更少的平均tokens

Conclusion: 简单提示检查清单可以改进LLM响应，同时减少不必要的交互，结构化提示设计确实能提高响应质量并降低用户工作量

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for open-ended tasks, but underspecified prompts can lead to low-quality answers and additional interaction. This paper studies whether structured prompt design improves response quality while reducing user effort. We compare three prompt conditions: a raw prompt, a checklist-improved prompt, and a clarifying-question prompt. We evaluate these conditions across four task types--summarization, planning, explanation, and coding--using three LLM systems: ChatGPT, Claude, and Grok. Each output is scored with a unified rubric covering task completion, correctness, compliance, and clarity. Checklist-improved prompts achieved the highest mean rubric score, 7.50 out of 8, compared with 5.67 for raw prompts and 6.67 for clarifying-question prompts. Checklist prompts also produced the best quality-effort tradeoff, using fewer average tokens than both raw and clarifying prompts. These results suggest that a simple prompt checklist can improve LLM responses while reducing unnecessary interaction.

</details>


### [154] [From Seeing to Thinking: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning Improves Post-Training of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20177)
*Juncheng Wu,Hardy Chen,Haoqin Tu,Xianfeng Tang,Freda Shi,Hui Liu,Hanqing Lu,Cihang Xie,Yuyin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过将视觉语言模型的训练分解为视觉感知、视觉推理和文本推理三个独立阶段，研究发现视觉感知是限制性能的主要瓶颈，并提出分阶段训练方法能显著提升模型性能，减少不必要的推理长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的视觉语言模型强调长链思维推理，但研究发现这些模型的视觉任务性能主要受限于视觉感知能力不足而非推理本身。需要系统研究感知与推理在VLM后训练中的相互作用，并探索更有效的训练方法。

Method: 提出分阶段训练方法，将VLM能力分解为三个独立训练阶段：1) 视觉感知训练；2) 视觉推理训练；3) 文本推理训练，每个阶段使用专门训练数据。研究表明视觉感知需要通过专门数据进行有针对性的优化，应作为基础支架通过分阶段训练巩固后再优化视觉推理，且视觉感知通过强化学习比基于描述的监督微调更有效学习。

Result: 在多个VLM上的实验表明，分阶段训练相比合并训练能持续提升视觉感知和推理性能。使用该方法训练的模型推理准确率提高1.5%，同时推理轨迹缩短20.8%，表明更好的感知能力减少了不必要的过度推理。这种基于能力的阶段划分与传统基于难度的课程学习正交，结合两者可获得额外的增益。分阶段训练模型在开放权重VLM中达到先进性能，在视觉数学和感知任务上相比基准模型有显著提升（WeMath +5.2%，RealWorldQA +3.7%）。

Conclusion: 视觉感知是VLM性能的关键限制因素，需要通过专门的分阶段训练方法进行系统优化。分阶段训练不仅提高了感知和推理性能，还减少了不必要的推理长度，表明改善感知能力可以简化推理过程。基于能力的阶段划分与基于难度的课程学习是互补的维度，为VLM训练提供了新的有效策略。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) emphasize long chain-of-thought reasoning; yet, we find that their performance on visual tasks is primarily limited by a lack of visual perception as opposed to reasoning itself. In this work, we systematically study the interplay between perception and reasoning in VLM post-training by decomposing their capabilities into three separate training stages: visual perception, visual reasoning, and textual reasoning, incorporating specialized training data. We demonstrate that visual perception (a) requires targeted optimization with specialized data; (b) serves as a fundamental scaffold that should be solidified through staged training before refining visual reasoning; and (c) is more effectively learned via RL than caption-based SFT. Our experiments across multiple VLMs demonstrate that staged training consistently improves both visual perception and reasoning performance over merged training. Notably, models trained with our approach achieve 1.5% higher reasoning accuracy with 20.8% shorter reasoning traces, suggesting that superior perception reduces the need for excessive reasoning. Furthermore, we show that this capability-based staging represents a new curriculum dimension orthogonal to traditional difficulty-based curricula, and combining both yields further additive gains. Our staged-training models achieve superior performance among open-weight VLMs, establishing advanced results on several visual math and perception (e.g., +5.2% on WeMath and +3.7% on RealWorldQA) tasks compared with the base counterpart.

</details>


### [155] [TIDE: Efficient and Lossless MoE Diffusion LLM Inference with I/O-aware Expert Offload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20179)
*Zhiben Chen,Youpeng Zhao,Yang Sui,Jun Wang,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TIDE是一种新颖的资源高效推理系统，专门为扩展的扩散大语言模型设计，通过利用扩散过程中专家激活的时间稳定性，采用间隔式专家刷新策略，实现无损失优化，在单GPU-CPU系统中可获得1.4-1.5倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着扩散大语言模型采用混合专家架构不断扩展，其在资源受限设备上的部署仍然是一个挑战。现有的自回归方法通常会产生过高的I/O开销或显著的计算瓶颈，需要更高效的推理系统。

Method: TIDE利用扩散过程中块内专家激活的时间稳定性，引入间隔式专家刷新策略，以I/O感知方式更新专家放置。通过将推理调度构建为数学规划问题，求解最小化I/O流量和CPU计算的最优间隔。该方法无需模型训练，是无损失优化。

Result: 在单GPU-CPU系统中，TIDE在LLaDA2.0-mini和LLaDA2.0-flash模型上分别实现了1.4倍和1.5倍的吞吐量提升，优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: TIDE是一种有效的资源高效推理系统，通过利用扩散过程中专家激活的时间稳定性特征，实现了对dLLM推理的无损失加速，为混合专家架构扩散模型在资源受限设备上的部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have emerged as a competitive alternative to autoregressive (AR) models, offering better hardware utilization and bidirectional context through parallel block-level decoding. However, as dLLMs continue to scale up with mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures, their deployment on resource-constrained devices remains an open challenge. Existing AR-based methods often incur either prohibitive I/O overhead or significant compute bottlenecks. In this work, we propose TIDE, a novel resource-efficient inference system that leverages the temporal stability of expert activations during the diffusion process within the block. Specifically, we leverage the temporal stability of expert activations during the diffusion process within the block and introduce an interval-based expert refresh strategy that updates the expert placement in an I/O-aware fashion. To ensure optimal performance, we formulate the inference scheduling as a mathematical programming problem, solving for the optimal interval that minimizes I/O traffic and CPU computation. Most importantly, TIDE is a lossless optimization that requires no model training, providing a "free lunch" acceleration for dLLM inference. In a single GPU-CPU system, we demonstrate that TIDE achieves up to 1.4$\times$ and 1.5$\times$ throughput improvements over prior baselines on LLaDA2.0-mini and LLaDA2.0-flash models, respectively.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Position: Let's Develop Data Probes to Fundamentally Understand How Data Affects LLM Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18801)
*Shiqiang Wang,Herbert Woisetschläger,Hans Arno Jacobsen,Mingyue Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"数据探针"方法，通过生成具有特定统计特性的合成序列来系统研究数据特性如何影响大语言模型的行为，为理解数据在LLM工作流程中的作用提供理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM数据研究主要依赖大规模公共数据集的实验经验，这种方法计算密集且缺乏系统理解数据特性如何驱动模型行为的理论基础。虽然数据对LLM至关重要，但特定数据特性在不同工作阶段（训练、微调、对齐、上下文学习等）的作用机制仍不明确。

Method: 提出"数据探针"方法：从适当定义的随机过程中生成合成序列，这些序列具有可控的统计特性。通过观察LLM在这些探针数据上的行为，系统研究数据特性如何影响模型性能、泛化能力和鲁棒性。探针序列的统计特性可以用典型集等理论概念来分析和描述。

Result: 数据探针方法为理解数据在LLM训练和推理中的作用提供了新的研究途径。这种方法能够超越经验启发式，通过可控的实验设置揭示数据特性与模型行为之间的系统性关系，为数据筛选和数据集构建提供理论基础。

Conclusion: 需要开发系统化的方法论来生成合成数据探针，这种方法能够提供对LLM数据需求更深刻的理论理解，减少对计算密集型实验的依赖，为数据在LLM工作流程中的角色建立更严谨的科学基础。

Abstract: Data is fundamental to large language models (LLMs). However, understanding of what makes certain data useful for different stages of an LLM workflow, including training, tuning, alignment, in-context learning, etc., and why, remains an open question. Current approaches rely heavily on extensive experimentation with large public datasets to obtain empirical heuristics for data filtering and dataset construction. These approaches are compute intensive and lack a principled way of understanding the essence of how specific data characteristics drive LLM behavior. In this position paper, we advocate for the need of developing systematic methodologies for generating synthetic sequences from appropriately defined random processes, with the goal that these sequences can reveal useful characteristics when they are used in one or multiple stages of the LLM workflow. We refer to such sequences as data probes. By observing LLM behavior on data probes, researchers can systematically conduct studies on how data characteristics influence model performance, generalization, and robustness. The probing sequences exhibit statistical properties that can be viewed using theoretical concepts, such as typical sets, which are generalized to describe the behaviors of LLMs. This data-probe approach provides a pathway for uncovering foundational insights into the role of data in LLM training and inference, beyond empirical heuristics.

</details>


### [157] [Operationalizing Document AI: A Microservice Architecture for OCR and LLM Pipelines in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18818)
*Yao Fehlis,Benjamin Bengfort,Zhangzhang Si,Vahid Eyorokon,Prema Roman,Patrick Deziel,Devon Slonaker,Steve Veldman,Ben Johnson,Joyce Rigelo,Michael Wharton,Steve Kramer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个用于文档理解的微服务架构，该架构封装了分类、OCR和LLM结构化字段提取的多模型流水线，实现了每小时处理数千份多页文档的生产级规模


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术研究通常关注新模型的定义，而在生产规模部署方面存在巨大差距。旨在填补文献中模型定义与实际生产部署之间的鸿沟，为实践者提供可行的架构模式

Method: 采用微服务架构，设计混合分类系统，分离GPU推理与CPU编排，使用异步处理处理IO密集型操作，实施独立水平扩展策略，并通过批量性能分析识别系统瓶颈

Result: 识别出两个重要发现：OCR（而非语言模型解析）主导端到端延迟；系统在达到GPU推理容量确定的并发度时饱和，而不是受限于工作节点数量

Conclusion: 该架构提供了实用的模式，使文档理解系统能够超越基准测试，在真实生产环境中有效运行，填补了学术研究与生产部署之间的关键差距

Abstract: Academic research tends to focus on new models for document understanding creating a wide gap in the literature between model definition and running models at production scale. To close that gap, we present a microservice architecture that encapsulates pipelines of multiple models for classification, optical character recognition (OCR), and large language model structured field extraction as well as our experience running this pipeline on thousands of multi-page documents per hour. We describe our primary design decisions, including a hybrid classification, separation of GPU-bound inference from CPU-bound orchestration, use of asynchronous processing for the many IO-bound operations in the pipeline, and an independent, horizontal scaling strategy. Using batch profiling, we identified two surprising qualitative findings that shape production deployments: OCR, not language-model parsing, dominates end-to-end latency, and the system saturates at a concurrency determined by shared GPU-inference capacity rather than worker count. Our goal is to provide practitioners with concrete architectural patterns for building document understanding systems that work beyond the benchmark; effectively operationalizing models in production.

</details>


### [158] [Evaluating the Utility of Personal Health Records in Personalized Health AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18937)
*Rory Sayres,Kejia Chen,Ayush Jain,Matthew Thompson,Jonathan Richina,Xiang Yin,Jimmy Hu,Fan Zhang,Bob Lou,Mike Sanchez,Ines Mezerreg,Meredith Schreier,Hamsa Subramaniam,I-Ching Lee,Yugang Jia,Daniel Mcduff,Yossi Matias,Avinatan Hassidim,Dale Webster,Yun Liu,Jackie Barr,Quang Duong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 评估大型语言模型（Gemini 3.0 Flash）结合个人健康记录数据回答患者健康查询的潜力，发现PHR上下文显著提升回答质量，同时开发了新的评估框架识别LLM在理解复杂PHR时的特定错误模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人健康记录信息复杂，可能阻碍患者获取健康洞察。研究旨在评估大型语言模型结合PHR临床数据为患者提供有用健康回答的潜力，以帮助患者更好地理解自己的健康状况。

Method: 研究从3种分布收集2,257个用户查询：简短网页搜索查询、较长聊天机器人对话模板问题、患者向医疗团队询问的问题。将查询与1,945个去标识化PHR匹配，使用Gemini模型生成三种条件下的回答：无PHR上下文、基本人口统计/病症/药物摘要、完整临床记录。采用SHARP评级框架和自主开发的新框架评估，使用自动评分（全数据集）和临床医生评分（95个样本子集）。

Result: 所有问题类型在获得PHR数据后回答质量均有显著提升（p < 0.001）。观察到安全性、准确性、相关性和个性化方面的潜在改进。新开发的PHR评估框架识别了LLM在理解复杂PHR时的特定问题，如时间错位和罕见但有意义的虚构内容。

Conclusion: PHR数据有潜力帮助各类用户更好地理解健康记录，该研究提供了监测LLM基于PHR上下文回答中缺陷的框架，并激励进一步研究如何实现用户从理解健康记录中获益。

Abstract: Patient-managed Personal Health Records (PHRs) promises to empower patients to better understand their health; but information in the record is complex, potentially hindering insights. In this study, we assess the potential of large language models (LLMs, Gemini 3.0 Flash) to provide helpful answers to user health queries, when provided clinical data from PHRs as context. A total of 2,257 user queries were drawn from 3 different distributions to represent patient questions: shorter web search queries, longer questions derived from templates of chatbot conversations, and questions patients asked to their healthcare team (patient calls). Queries were matched with de-identified PHRs (from a pool of 1,945). Gemini responses were generated (1) without PHR context; (2) with a basic summary of demographics, conditions, and medications; (3) with full, extensive clinical notes. For evaluation, we leveraged an existing rating framework (SHARP), and developed a new framework for specific error modes when interpreting PHRs. Evaluation was performed using autoraters for the full set, and with clinician ratings for a subset (n=95), with both sets of raters knowing the full PHR context. We see significant improvements in the helpfulness of answers to all question types with PHR data (p < 0.001, paired t-test). We also observe potential gains in safety, accuracy, relevance and personalization of answers. Our PHR evaluation framework further identifies gaps in LLM understanding of particular aspects of complex PHRs, such as temporal disorientation, and rare but meaningful confabulations. These results suggest potential for PHR data to help people with a wide range of user needs; and provide a framework for monitoring for gaps in LLM answers based on PHR context. This study motivates further work to assess and realize potential benefits to users from understanding their health records.

</details>


### [159] [Learn-by-Wire Training Control Governance: Bounded Autonomous Training Under Stress for Stability and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19008)
*Anis Radianis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LBW-Guard是一个在AdamW之上运行的有限自主训练控制治理层，通过观察训练遥测数据、解释不稳定性敏感机制，在保持固定训练目标的同时对优化器执行应用有界控制，显著提高语言模型训练的稳定性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型训练在激进的学习率、规模和运行时压力条件下越来越容易暴露于不稳定性、训练退化和计算资源浪费的问题，需要一种能够提高训练稳健性的解决方案。

Method: LBW-Guard不是替换优化器更新规则，而是在AdamW之上构建一个有界自主训练控制治理层。它观察训练遥测数据，识别不稳定性敏感机制，对优化器执行应用有界控制，同时保持固定的训练目标。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B参考设置中，LBW-Guard将最终困惑度从13.21降低到10.74（18.7%改进），端到端时间从392.54秒减少到357.02秒（1.10倍加速）。在更强的学习率压力下，AdamW在LR=3e-3时退化到1885.24困惑度，而LBW-Guard保持在11.57可训练状态。梯度裁剪基线无法复现这一效果。

Conclusion: 稳定性敏感的大型语言模型训练可以从优化器之上的治理平面中受益。LBW-Guard证明了有界运行时控制能够在压力下保持高效计算，同时与优化器替换和局部梯度抑制保持区别。

Abstract: Modern language-model training is increasingly exposed to instability, degraded runs, and wasted compute, especially under aggressive learning-rate, scale, and runtime-stress conditions. This paper introduces Learn-by-Wire Guard (LBW-Guard), a bounded autonomous training-control governance layer that operates above AdamW. Rather than replacing the optimizer update rule, LBW-Guard observes training telemetry, interprets instability-sensitive regimes, and applies bounded control to optimizer execution while preserving fixed training objectives.
  We evaluate LBW-Guard in a Qwen2.5-centered stress-and-robustness suite using WikiText-103, with Qwen2.5-7B as the empirical anchor, model-size comparisons against Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-14B, learning-rate stress tests, gradient-clipping baselines, and a no-LoRA TinyLlama-1B full-parameter sanity check. In the 7B reference setting, LBW-Guard reduces final perplexity from 13.21 to 10.74, an 18.7% improvement, while reducing end-to-end time from 392.54s to 357.02s, a 1.10x speedup. Under stronger learning-rate stress, AdamW degrades to 1885.24 final perplexity at LR=3e-3 and 659.76 at LR=1e-3, whereas LBW-Guard remains trainable at 11.57 and 10.33, respectively. Gradient-clipping baselines do not reproduce this effect.
  These results support a scoped systems conclusion that stability-sensitive LLM training can benefit from a governance plane above the optimizer. LBW-Guard provides evidence that bounded runtime control can preserve productive compute under stress while remaining distinct from optimizer replacement and local gradient suppression.

</details>


### [160] [DecisionBench: A Benchmark for Emergent Delegation in Long-Horizon Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19099)
*Yuxuan Gao,Megan Wang,Yi Ling Yu,Zijian Carl Ma,Ao Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DecisionBench是一个用于长视野智能体工作流中涌现委派评估的基准平台，包含任务套件、模型池、委派接口和多元指标，为学习路由器和自适应委派方法提供评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前长视野智能体工作流中缺乏系统评估涌现委派能力的标准基准，现有的评估通常只关注任务质量而忽略委派编排信号，需要更全面的评估框架来推动委派策略的研究发展。

Method: 设计了DecisionBench基准平台，包括：1）任务套件（GAIA, tau-bench, BFCL多轮任务）；2）对等模型池（11个模型，7个供应商）；3）委派接口（call_model加可选read_profile通道）；4）确定性技能标注层；5）多元指标套件（质量、成本、延迟、委派率、路由准确度、供应商自我偏好、反事实委派上限）。

Result: 通过对完整模型池进行五种条件的基准测试（n=23,375任务实例）发现：1）四种感知条件下的平均任务质量统计无差异（|β|≤0.010，p≥0.21）；2）路由准确度（fidelity-at-1）在7.5%到29.5%之间变化，传递通道（按需工具vs预加载描述）的影响超过描述内容本身；3）反事实上限显示完美委派比当前测量性能高出15-31个百分点。

Conclusion: DecisionBench为涌现委派评估提供了全面基准，揭示了单纯质量评估会遗漏编排信号，且存在显著性能提升空间，已发布完整平台、标注层、参考干预套件和分析流水线供研究社区使用。

Abstract: We introduce DecisionBench, a benchmark substrate for emergent delegation in long-horizon agentic workflows. The substrate fixes a task suite (GAIA, tau-bench, BFCL multi-turn), a peer-model pool (11 models, 7 vendor families), a delegation interface (call_model plus an optional read_profile channel), a deterministic skill-annotation layer, and a multi-axis metric suite covering quality, cost, latency, delegation rate, routing fidelity-at-k, vendor self-preference, and a counterfactual-delegation ceiling. The substrate is agnostic to how peer information is generated or delivered, so learned routers, richer peer memories, adaptive profile construction, and multi-step delegation can all be evaluated against it. We characterize the substrate with a five-condition reference sweep on the full pool (n=23,375 task instances). Three benchmark-level findings emerge: (i) mean end-task quality is statistically indistinguishable across the four awareness conditions (|beta| <= 0.010, p >= 0.21), so quality-only evaluation would miss the orchestration signal; (ii) routing fidelity-at-1 ranges from 7.5% to 29.5% across conditions at near-equal mean quality, with delivery channel (on-demand tool vs. preloaded description) dominating description content; (iii) a counterfactual ceiling places perfect delegation 15-31 percentage points above measured performance on every suite, locating large unrealized headroom for future orchestration methods. We release the substrate, annotation layer, reference intervention suite, analysis pipeline, and 220 per-condition run archives.

</details>


### [161] [Not all uncertainty is alike: volatility, stochasticity, and exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19215)
*Payam Piray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究了自适应决策中的不确定性探索不对称性：环境中的波动性和随机性虽然都增加后验不确定性，但对最优探索的影响方向相反——波动性增强探索，随机性抑制探索。作者开发了CAUSE探索策略，在异质噪声环境中优于标准方法，并对计算精神病学有理论启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统研究通常将不同类型的环境不确定性视为等同，都认为不确定性促进探索。但本文指出，波动性（latent reward states that drift over time）和随机性（observed through noisy outcomes）这两种不确定性虽然都增加后验不确定性，可能对探索产生不同的影响，这一差异尚未得到深入研究。

Method: 1. 将Gittins索引框架扩展到具有潜在动态的高斯状态空间多臂赌博机问题中。2. 推导出Cause-Aware Uncertainty-Sensitive Exploration (CAUSE)策略，这是一个通过控制即推理得到的封闭形式探索奖励，继承了相同的不对称单调性。3. 比较CAUSE与标准探索策略在异质噪声结构环境中的性能。4. 分析Gittins-per-arm策略在非静态设置中的局限性。

Result: 1. 理论上证明波动性和随机性对最优探索产生相反影响：波动性增强探索，随机性抑制探索。2. CAUSE策略在异质噪声环境中优于标准探索策略。3. Gittins-per-arm策略在静态赌博机问题中的最优性不能推广到非静态设置中。4. 学习和探索都受到相同的噪声推理不对称性控制，该框架预测病理性噪声推理会产生反向而非仅仅是受损的探索行为。

Conclusion: 环境不确定性的来源（波动性与随机性）对探索行为具有根本不同的影响，这一不对称性揭示了自适应决策的深层机制。CAUSE策略通过明确区分不确定性的来源来优化探索。这一发现对计算精神病学模型有重要启示，表明某些精神状况可能源于对噪声来源的误解而导致的反向探索行为，而不仅仅是探索受损。

Abstract: Adaptive decision-making in biological and artificial intelligence requires balancing the exploitation of known outcomes with the exploration of uncertain alternatives. Although prior work suggests that uncertainty generally promotes exploration, it has typically treated distinct sources of environmental uncertainty as equivalent. We consider environments with latent reward states that drift over time (volatility) and are observed through noisy outcomes (stochasticity). Both increase posterior uncertainty, yet we show they drive optimal exploration in opposite directions: volatility enhances it, stochasticity suppresses it. We establish this asymmetry formally by extending the Gittins index framework to Gaussian state-space bandits with latent dynamics. We further derive Cause-Aware Uncertainty-Sensitive Exploration (CAUSE), a closed-form exploration bonus obtained via control-as-inference that inherits the same monotonicities. CAUSE outperforms standard exploration strategies in environments with heterogeneous noise structure, and also improves on a Gittins-per-arm policy whose rested-bandit optimality does not transfer to restless settings. Learning and exploration are governed by the same noise-inference asymmetry, and the framework predicts that pathological noise inference produces \emph{reversed} rather than merely impaired exploration, with implications for computational accounts of psychiatric conditions.

</details>


### [162] [Can Large Language Models Revolutionize Survey Research? Experiments with Disaster Preparedness Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19229)
*Yan Wang,Ziyi Guo,Christopher McCarty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个五阶段框架，将大型语言模型（LLMs）整合到调查研究中，以应对传统调查面临的低响应率、样本偏差和AI辅助欺诈等挑战。通过在2024年飓风米爾顿准备情况调查中测试，提出的锚定边际理论知情LLM（A-TLM）在数据插补性能上超越了传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统调查研究面临结构性挑战：响应率下降、样本偏差、高风险受访者中的块状缺失数据，以及在线面板中的AI辅助欺诈完成。LLMs被提议作为解决方案，但在整个调查工作流程中缺乏严谨评估，特别是在数据质量至关重要的灾害情境中。

Method: 提出了一个五阶段LLM整合框架：问卷设计、样本选择、试点测试、缺失数据插补和后收集分析。使用2024年佛罗里达州居民飓风米爾顿准备情况调查（n=946）作为实证测试平台。引入了保护动机理论（PMT）约束的共现知识图谱，开发了七种LLM配置，包括零样本推断、检索增强基线和新的理论知情变体。

Result: 提出的锚定边际理论知情LLM（A-TLM）在灾害相关的块状MNAR条件下，在所有三种经典插补基线（IPW/MI、MICE+PMM、missForest）上均表现最佳（S4 RMSE 1.439 vs. 次优的1.496）。实现了接近零的有符号偏差（-0.121），而随机森林插补器产生最大绝对偏差（-0.631）。围绕PMT因果结构组织的检索以及将所有证据整合在单次模型调用中，优于非结构化检索和分阶段顺序推断（MAE 0.993 vs. 标准RAG的1.097）。

Conclusion: 研究表明LLMs可以提升调查研究的质量，特别是在灾害情境下。提出的A-TLM方法在数据插补方面优于传统方法。研究发现接近零的总体偏差可能掩盖了相反的亚组错误，提出了亚组分层的偏差审计作为报告标准。通过基于知识图谱的聊天机器人展示了通过接地拒绝可以有效管理幻觉问题。

Abstract: Survey research faces mounting structural challenges: declining response rates, sample bias, block-wise missingness among at-risk respondents, and AI-assisted fraudulent completions in online panels. Large language models (LLMs) have been proposed as a remedy, yet rigorous evaluations across the full survey workflow remain scarce, particularly in disaster contexts where data quality matters most. We present and evaluate a five-stage framework for LLM integration covering questionnaire design, sample selection, pilot testing, missing-data imputation, and post-collection analysis, using the 2024 Hurricane Milton preparedness survey of Florida residents (n=946) as a shared empirical testbed. We introduce a Protection Motivation Theory (PMT)-constrained co-occurrence knowledge graph and develop seven LLM configurations spanning zero-shot inference, retrieval-augmented baselines, and novel theory-informed variants. Our proposed Anchored Marginal Theory-Informed LLM (A-TLM) outperforms all three classical imputation baselines (IPW/MI, MICE+PMM, missForest) on RMSE under disaster-relevant block-wise MNAR conditions (S4 RMSE 1.439 vs. 1.496 for the next-best), while achieving near-zero signed bias (-0.121) where the random-forest imputer produces the largest absolute bias (-0.631). Organizing retrieval around PMT causal structure and integrating all evidence in a single model call outperforms unstructured retrieval and staged sequential inference (MAE 0.993 vs. 1.097 for standard RAG). We document that near-zero aggregate bias can mask opposing subgroup errors and propose subgroup-stratified bias auditing as a reporting standard. A retrieval-constrained knowledge-graph chatbot demonstrates that hallucination is architecturally manageable through grounded refusal.

</details>


### [163] [Causal Evidence for Attention Head Imbalance in Modality Conflict Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19250)
*Jinrui Jiang,Zhangtai Wu,Zhen Wu,Xinyu Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TLDR：该研究通过机制视角分析多模态大语言模型中的模态冲突幻觉现象，识别出驱动和抵抗幻觉的注意力头，并提出基于因果干预的MACI方法来降低幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模态冲突幻觉是指多模态大语言模型优先考虑错误的文本前提而非矛盾的视觉证据。研究动机是理解为何视觉证据在生成过程中无法占主导地位，从机制角度分析哪些内部组件驱动或抵抗这种失败。

Method: 采用机制视角，对五个开源MLLM进行头级因果分析，使用路径补丁技术识别具有相反因果作用的注意力头：幻觉驱动头和幻觉抵抗头。发现不对称模式：驱动效应分布更广、总权重更大，而抵抗效应集中在少数高重要性头中。基于此提出MACI方法（模态冲突感知因果干预），仅在检测到冲突时抑制因果识别的幻觉驱动头。

Result: 在五个MLLM上的实验显示，MACI在MMMC基准测试中实现了最大的幻觉减少，在幻觉-准确性权衡方面表现良好，并能零样本迁移到SCI-SemanticConflict测试。

Conclusion: 研究揭示MLLM中存在不对称的路由结构，其中分布的驱动影响和局部化的抵抗共同偏向于错误前提的生成。MACI方法通过条件性抑制驱动头，有效减少了模态冲突幻觉，为理解和改善MLLM的可靠性提供了新途径。

Abstract: Modality-conflict hallucination occurs when multimodal large language models (MLLMs) prioritize erroneous textual premises over contradictory visual evidence. To understand why visual evidence fails to prevail during generation, we take a mechanistic perspective and examine which internal components drive or resist this failure. We perform head-level causal analysis using path patching across five open-source MLLMs and identify two groups of attention heads with opposing causal roles: hallucination-driving heads and hallucination-resisting heads. We find a consistent asymmetry: driving effects are more broadly distributed and carry greater aggregate weight, whereas resisting effects concentrate in a small number of high-importance heads. Ablation experiments further confirm that these groups exert opposing effects during generation: distributed driving influence and localized resistance together form an imbalanced routing structure that biases generation toward the erroneous premise. Motivated by this finding, we propose MACI (Modality-conflict-Aware Causal Intervention), a conditional intervention that suppresses causally identified hallucination-driving heads only when conflict is detected. Across five MLLMs, MACI achieves the largest hallucination reduction among compared inference-time baselines on the MMMC benchmark with a favorable hallucination-accuracy trade-off, and transfers zero-shot to the SCI-SemanticConflict test.

</details>


### [164] [Swimming with Whales: Analysis of Power Imbalances in Stake-Weighted Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19264)
*Yuzhe Zhang,Manvir Schneider,Qin Wang,Davide Grossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TL;DR: 本文研究权益证明区块链中的股权加权投票系统，使用彭罗斯-班扎夫权力指数分析权力失衡现象，证明了权力与持股比例的完美对齐通常无法实现，但可在特定条件下近似实现，并通过真实链上治理系统数据进行了实证分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 股权加权投票是权益证明区块链的基础治理范式，但这种模式容易产生权力扭曲：少数拥有大量股权的用户可能完全控制决策，即使他们并不拥有全部股权。研究者希望通过计算社会选择理论的视角，量化这种权力失衡的程度。

Method: 采用计算社会选择理论的方法，特别是使用彭罗斯-班扎夫权力指数来量化权力失衡。研究包含分析和实证两部分：理论上证明权力与相对股权所有权之间的关系；实证上使用真实链上治理系统（Project Catalyst）的数据来分析实际发生的权力失衡。

Result: 分析结果表明：1）权力与相对股权所有权的完美对齐通常无法实现；2）在特定条件下，这种对齐可以在期望值上近似实现；3）通过对真实数据的实证分析，提供了当前股权加权治理系统中可能发生的更细粒度的权力失衡理解。

Conclusion: 股权加权投票系统存在固有的权力失衡风险，少数大股东可能不成比例地影响决策。虽然完美平衡难以实现，但在某些条件下可以近似。研究强调了对当前区块链治理系统进行更细致权力分析的重要性，为改进治理机制提供了理论基础和实证依据。

Abstract: Voting methods weighted by stakes are the fundamental governance paradigm in Proof-of-Stake (PoS) blockchains. Such a paradigm is known to be prone to power distortions: a few users possessing large stakes may completely control decision making, even without owning the totality of the stakes. We study this phenomenon through the lens of computational social choice, focusing on the extent of power imbalances in stake-weighted voting when power is quantified using the Penrose-Banzhaf power index. Our work presents both analytical and empirical contributions. Analytically, we demonstrate that while a perfect alignment between power and relative stake ownership is generally unattainable, it can be approximated in expectation under specific conditions. Empirically, using data from a real-world on-chain governance system (Project Catalyst), we provide a more fine-grained understanding of the power imbalances that are likely to occur in current stake-weighted governance systems.

</details>


### [165] [Generative Recursive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19376)
*Junyeob Baek,Mingyu Jo,Minsu Kim,Mengye Ren,Yoshua Bengio,Sungjin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRAM（生成式递归推理模型）将递归潜在推理转化为概率多轨迹计算，通过随机潜在轨迹建模推理过程，支持多假设、替代解决方案，并能通过递归深度和平行轨迹采样进行推理时扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的递归推理模型（RRMs）大多是确定性的，遵循单一的潜在轨迹并收敛到单一预测。这限制了模型探索多种假设和替代解决方案的能力，无法实现概率性的多轨迹计算。

Method: 提出GRAM框架，将推理建模为随机潜在轨迹，使用分摊变分推断进行训练。该方法支持条件推理（p_θ(y|x)）和无条件生成（p_θ(x)），可通过递归深度和平行轨迹采样进行扩展。

Result: 在结构化推理和多解约束满足任务上，GRAM优于确定性的循环和递归基线模型，同时展示了无条件生成能力。

Conclusion: GRAM为神经推理系统提供了一种有前景的概率多轨迹计算方法，克服了现有确定性递归推理模型的局限性，实现了更灵活的推理过程和更好的性能。

Abstract: How should future neural reasoning systems implement extended computation? Recursive Reasoning Models (RRMs) offer a promising alternative to autoregressive sequence extension by performing iterative latent-state refinement with shared transition functions. Yet existing RRMs are largely deterministic, following a single latent trajectory and converging to a single prediction. We introduce \emph{Generative Recursive reAsoning Models (GRAM)}, a framework that turns recursive latent reasoning into probabilistic multi-trajectory computation. GRAM models reasoning as a stochastic latent trajectory, enabling multiple hypotheses, alternative solution strategies, and inference-time scaling through both recursive depth and parallel trajectory sampling. This yields a latent-variable generative model supporting conditional reasoning via $p_θ(y \mid x)$ and, with fixed or absent inputs, unconditional generation via $p_θ(x)$. Trained with amortized variational inference, GRAM improves over deterministic recurrent and recursive baselines on structured reasoning and multi-solution constraint satisfaction tasks, while demonstrating an unconditional generation capability. \href{https://ahn-ml.github.io/gram-website/}{https://ahn-ml.github.io/gram-website}

</details>


### [166] [Beyond Mode Collapse: Distribution Matching for Diverse Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19461)
*Xiaozhe Li,Yang Li,Xinyu Fang,Shengyuan Ding,Peiji Li,Yongkang Chen,Yichuan Ma,Tianyi Lyu,Linyang Li,Dahua Lin,Qipeng Guo,Qingwen Liu,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DMPO（分布匹配策略优化）通过前向KL最小化的原则性近似来防止模式崩塌，在组合优化任务中实现比GRPO更高的质量比，并展示跨任务的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于策略的强化学习方法（如GRPO）存在模式崩塌问题：它们会减少解决方案的多样性，一旦发现一个高奖励解就集中概率质量，停止探索替代策略。这源于反向KL最小化的模式寻求行为。

Method: 提出DMPO（分布匹配策略优化），通过构造基于采样轨迹奖励的群体级目标分布，然后将策略分布与该目标对齐，实现前向KL最小化的原则性近似。这种方法提供模式覆盖行为，无需从难以处理的全局目标分布中采样。

Result: 在NP难组合优化测试中，DMPO在文本NP-Bench上达到43.9%质量比（vs. GRPO的40.1%），在视觉NP-Bench上达到43.1%（vs. 38.4%），分别相对提升9%和12%。这些改进泛化到数学推理（+2.0%）和域外任务（+2.3%）。

Conclusion: 分布匹配是防止基于策略强化学习中模式崩塌的实用原则性方法，多样性的保持训练能够增强跨模态的通用推理能力，持续探索带来一致的质量提升。

Abstract: On-policy reinforcement learning methods like GRPO suffer from mode collapse: they exhibit reduced solution diversity, concentrating probability mass on a single solution once discovered and ceasing exploration of alternative strategies. We show this stems from reverse KL minimization's mode-seeking behavior, which reinforces the first high-reward trajectory found rather than maintaining a distribution over multiple diverse solutions. We propose DMPO (Distribution-Matching Policy Optimization), which prevents mode collapse through principled approximation of forward KL minimization. DMPO constructs a group level target distribution over sampled trajectories proportional to their rewards, then aligns the policy distribution to this target. This provides mode-covering behavior without requiring sampling from the intractable global target distribution, enabling sustained exploration throughout training. We validate DMPO on NP-hard combinatorial optimization, where exponentially many feasible solutions exist but only a few approach optimality, an ideal testbed for evaluating exploration. DMPO achieves 43.9% Quality Ratio on text-based NP-Bench (vs. GRPO's 40.1%) and 43.1% on vision-based NP-Bench (vs. 38.4%), demonstrating 9% and 12% relative improvements respectively. These gains generalize to mathematical reasoning (+2.0%) and out-of-domain tasks (+2.3%), showing that diversity-preserving training enhances general reasoning capabilities across modalities. Our work establishes distribution matching as a practical, principled approach to preventing mode collapse in on-policy RL, with consistent quality improvements demonstrating sustained exploration across diverse reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [167] [Attention-Guided Reward for Reinforcement Learning-based Jailbreak against Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19485)
*Zheng Lin,Zhenxing Niu,Haoxuan Ji,Yuzhe Huang,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的LRMs越狱攻击方法，通过将注意力信号纳入奖励函数设计，并结合多样化的说服策略，显著提高了攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRMs）在处理复杂问题时展现出卓越能力，但暴露内部推理过程会引入安全风险，研究发现LRMs比标准LLMs更容易受到越狱攻击。研究揭示攻击成功率与LRMs的注意力模式密切相关：成功的越狱倾向于对输入提示中的有害令牌分配较低注意力，而对推理内容中的这些令牌分配较高注意力。

Method: 提出一种新颖的LRMs越狱方法，利用强化学习增强攻击效果，将注意力信号明确纳入奖励函数设计。同时引入多样化的说服策略来丰富RL动作空间。

Result: 在五个开源和闭源LRM以及三个基准测试上的大量实验表明，该方法获得了显著更高的攻击成功率（ASR），在有效性、效率和可迁移性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过结合注意力分析和强化学习，成功开发了一种高效的LRMs越狱攻击框架，揭示了注意力模式与模型漏洞之间的关联，为LRMs的安全防御提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in solving complex problems by generating structured, step-by-step reasoning content. However, exposing a model's internal reasoning process introduces additional safety risks; for example, recent studies show that LRMs are more vulnerable to jailbreak attacks than standard LLMs. In this paper, we investigate jailbreak attacks on LRMs and reveal that the attack success rate (ASR) is closely correlated with LRMs' attention patterns. Specifically, successful jailbreaks tend to assign lower attention to harmful tokens in the input prompt, while allocating higher attention to those tokens in the reasoning content. Motivated by this finding, we propose a novel jailbreak method for LRMs that leverages reinforcement learning (RL) to enhance attack effectiveness, explicitly incorporating attention signals into the reward function design. In addition, we introduce diverse persuasion strategies to enrich the RL action space, which consistently improves the ASR. Extensive experiments on five open-source and closed-source LRMs across three benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves substantially higher ASR, outperforming existing approaches in terms of effectiveness, efficiency, and transferability.

</details>


### [168] [Position: The Turing-Completeness of Real-World Autoregressive Transformers Relies Heavily on Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19514)
*Guanyu Cui,Zhewei Wei,Kun He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Many works make the eye-catching claim that Transformers are Turing-complete. However, the literature often conflates two distinct settings: (i) a fixed Transformer system setting, in which a fixed autoregressive Transformer is coupled with a fixed context-management method to process inputs of different lengths step by step, and (ii) a scaling-family setting, in which a family of different models (with increasing context-window length or numerical precision) is used to handle different input lengths. Existing proofs of Transformer Turing-completeness are frequently established in setting (ii), whereas real-world LLM deployment and the standard notion of Turing-completeness correspond more naturally to setting (i). In this paper, we first formalize the fixed-system setting, thereby providing a concrete characterization of how real-world LLMs operate. We then argue that results proved in the scaling-family setting provide theoretically meaningful resource bounds but do not establish Turing-completeness, thereby clarifying a common misinterpretation of existing results. Finally, we show that different context-management methods can yield sharply different computational power, and we advocate the position that context management is a central component that critically determines the computational power of real-world autoregressive Transformers.

</details>


### [169] [BLINKG: A Benchmark for LLM-Integrated Knowledge Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19518)
*Carla Castedo,Enrique Iglesias,Manuel Lama,Alberto Bugarin-Diz,Maria-Esther Vidal,David Chaves-Fraga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Generating Knowledge Graphs (KGs) remains one of the most time-consuming and labor-intensive tasks for knowledge engineers, as they need to identify semantic equivalences between input data sources and ontology terms. While declarative solutions (e.g., RML, SPARQL-Anything) have helped to generalize this process, aligning input schema elements with ontology terms still involves intricate transformations and requires considerable manual effort. With the advent of Large Language Models (LLMs), there is growing interest in leveraging their capabilities to assist KG engineers. Although some studies have explored using LLMs to automate KG construction, there is still no standardized framework for assessing how effectively they establish correspondences between data schemes and ontology concepts. Therefore, in this paper, we propose BLINKG, a benchmark designed to evaluate the mapping capabilities of LLMs in constructing KGs from heterogeneous data sources. The benchmark includes a set of scenarios with increasing complexity, based on real-world use cases. We conduct an extensive experimental evaluation of several stateof-the-art LLMs using BLINK and observe that they already offer promising solutions. However, their performance remains limited in complex scenarios. Thanks to this benchmark, we can already assess the current capabilities of LLMs for KG construction. Additionally, we define a set of requirements for achieving (semi)automated (LLM-driven) KG construction, opening new research lines in this area.

</details>


### [170] [Efficient Elicitation of Collective Disagreements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19521)
*Mohamed Ouaguenouni,Felipe Garrido-Lucero,Umberto Grandi,César Hidalgo,Magdalena Tydrichova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种分层框架，通过引入"多数矩阵"（记录每个子集中每个候选项排名第一的概率）来分析选民之间的结构性分歧，证明了许多现有的分歧度量（如排名方差和分裂度）需要至少3个候选项的子集信息，而不仅仅基于成对比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统调查方法要么使用成对比较（简单直观），要么要求完整的排名顺序（获取全部偏好信息）。成对比较无法区分结构性分歧和随机噪声，需要一种更好的方法来识别计算分歧度量所需的最小偏好信息。

Method: 提出分层框架，引入多数矩阵（plurality matrix）作为成对比较的推广，记录每个子集S中每个候选项a在S中排名第一的概率。定义分歧度量的"层级"为表达该度量所需的最小子集大小。设计了两种启发协议来估计多数矩阵，在参与者数量和认知负担之间权衡。

Result: 证明了许多现有分歧度量（包括排名方差和分裂度）位于第3级，表明成对比较（第2级）不足够。理论上和实验上展示了超越第3级的价值。设计的启发协议能有效估计多数矩阵。

Conclusion: 成对比较无法充分捕捉选民间的结构性分歧，需要至少考虑3个候选项的子集信息。提出的分层框架和多数矩阵为理解分歧结构提供了更精确的工具，两种启发协议使这些理论结果在实际应用中可行。

Abstract: We analyze the structure of the disagreement among a population of voters over a set of alternatives. Surveys typically ask either for pairwise comparisons, simple and intuitive for participants, or full rankings over alternatives, eliciting the entire voters' preferences. Building on the observation that pairwise comparisons cannot distinguish structural disagreement from noise, we propose a stratified framework to identify the minimal aggregated preference information needed to compute a number of disagreement measures from the literature. Specifically, we introduce the plurality matrix, a generalization of pairwise comparisons that records, for every subset $S$ of alternatives, the probability that each $a \in S$ ranks first in $S$. We define the level of a disagreement measure as the smallest subset size needed to express it, showing that many existing notions, including rank-variance and divisiveness, sit at level $3$, proving that pairwise comparisons are not enough. In addition, we demonstrate the interest of going beyond level $3$ both theoretically and experimentally. To make these results actionable, we design two elicitation protocols to estimate the plurality matrix, exploring the trade-off between the number of required participants and the cognitive load requested to each of them.

</details>


### [171] [Generative-Evaluative Agreement: A Necessary Validity Criterion for LLM-Enabled Adaptive Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19529)
*Grandee Lee,Yue Wang,Che Yee Lye,Luke Peh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出生成-评估一致性（GEA）作为衡量LLM在生成评估项目、模拟学生反应并评分这一自我验证循环的有效性标准，发现模型仅能恢复约一半的预期方差（r=0.698），并对低技能学生存在系统性高估


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当同一个LLM既生成评估项目，又模拟学生反应并进行评分时，形成了一个自我指涉的验证循环。需要建立一个有效性标准来衡量这种循环的可靠性，因为缺乏外部基准来验证LLM生成的评估的质量

Method: 引入生成-评估一致性（GEA）作为有效性标准，测量LLM的评分函数能否恢复其生成函数被指示产生的技能水平。在首个对两阶段自适应评估的GEA直接测量中，使用统计分析相关系数等方法

Result: 模型仅恢复约一半的预期方差（r=0.698），存在系统性正偏差。GEA在语法可验证技能上较强（r>0.7），但在设计级技能上接近零。低技能高估在路由阈值附近导致分数膨胀

Conclusion: 精细、技能分解的评分标准是增强GEA的主要机制，作者还概述了互补的缓解措施。需要更严格的验证框架来确保LLM生成评估的有效性和可靠性

Abstract: When the same LLM generates assessment items, simulates student responses, and scores them, the validation loop is self-referential. We introduce Generative-Evaluative Agreement (GEA), a validity criterion measuring whether an LLM's scoring function recovers the skill levels its generative function was instructed to produce. In the first direct measurement of GEA on a two-stage adaptive assessment, the model recovers roughly half the intended variance r = 0.698 with systematic positive bias. GEA is strong r > 0.7 for syntactically verifiable skills but near zero for design-level skills, and low-skill overestimation inflates scores near the routing threshold. We argue that granular, skill-decomposed rubrics are the principal proposed mechanism for strengthening GEA and outline complementary mitigations.

</details>


### [172] [SceneCode: Executable World Programs for Editable Indoor Scenes with Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19587)
*Puyi Wang,Yuhao Wang,Linjie Li,Zhengyuan Yang,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Yangguang Li,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SceneCode：一个将自然语言提示编译为可执行代码驱动室内世界的框架，通过程序化世界生成实现物理可交互的室内场景合成，替代传统的静态网格表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内场景合成方法通常将生成内容表示为静态网格，并从策划的资产库中继承关节结构，这限制了对象级可控性，无法按需生成新的可交互资产，阻碍了具身AI、机器人操作和基于仿真的策略评估应用。

Method: 提出程序化世界生成框架SceneCode：1）房间级智能骨干将自然语言提示转换为结构化房屋布局，通过规划师-设计师-批评家循环发出每个对象的资产请求；2）每个请求路由到五种代码生成策略之一，转换为合成的分部分Blender Python程序；3）通过执行引导的修复-优化循环验证程序；4）编译为仿真就绪资产并导出为SDF格式；5）持久化场景状态注册表链接对象请求、可执行程序、渲染几何和仿真资产。

Result: SceneCode在场景级合成、对象级资产质量、人类判断和下游机器人交互方面的评估显示：可执行世界程序提高了提示忠实度的室内场景生成，产生具有更清晰网格结构的资产，以及可加载到仿真器中的关节元数据。

Conclusion: SceneCode通过将室内场景合成重新构想为程序化世界生成，解决了现有静态网格表示的局限性，使场景组装成为可追踪和本地可编辑的世界构建过程，为具身AI和机器人应用提供了更好的可控性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Indoor scene synthesis underpins embodied AI, robotic manipulation, and simulation-based policy evaluation, where a useful scene must specify not only what the environment looks like, but also how its objects are structured. Existing pipelines, however, typically represent generated content as static meshes and inherit articulation only from curated asset libraries, which limits object-level controllability and prevents new interactable assets from being produced on demand. We address this gap by formulating physically interactable indoor scene synthesis as programmatic world generation, and present SceneCode, a framework that compiles a natural language prompt into an executable, code-driven indoor world rather than a collection of opaque meshes. A room-level agentic backbone first turns the prompt into a structured house layout and emits per-object AssetRequests through a planner--designer--critic loop. Each request is then routed to one of five code-generation strategies and converted into a synthesized part-wise Blender Python programs that are validated through an execution-guided repair-and-refine loop. The resulting programs are compiled into simulation-ready assets, and exported as SDF for physics simulation. A persistent scene-state registry links object requests, executable programs, rendered geometry, and simulation assets, turning scene assembly into a traceable and locally editable world-building process. We evaluate SceneCode across scene-level synthesis, object-level asset quality, human judgment, and downstream robot interaction. Results show that executable world programs improve prompt-faithful indoor scene generation and produce assets with cleaner mesh structure, and simulator-loadable articulation metadata. Project page: https://scene-code.github.io/.

</details>


### [173] [Towards Multi-Model LLM Schedulers: Empirical Insights into Offloading and Preemption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19593)
*Mert Yildiz,Pietro Spadaccino,Alexey Rolich,Francesca Cuomo,Andrea Baiocchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型语言模型多模型服务中的CPU-GPU混合卸载与抢占调度问题研究


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM部署需要在异构硬件上服务多个具有不同架构、规模和专业化的模型，这带来了新的资源分配、调度和分派挑战，特别是在GPU内存限制下需要部分CPU-GPU卸载和抢占。现有系统主要优化单个模型的吞吐量，而多模型调度研究相对不足。

Method: 对不同LLM在硬件平台上的行为进行实证研究，重点关注层卸载和抢占对性能的影响，分析卸载导致的解码吞吐量非线性退化、抢占开销以及模型状态重新加载与键值缓存传输的成本差异。

Result: 卸载会导致解码吞吐量出现强烈的非线性和模型依赖性退化，小模型对减少GPU驻留更为敏感；抢占产生显著开销，主要由模型状态重新加载而非键值缓存传输主导，且成本因模型和硬件平台而异；序列长度和互连带宽会放大数据移动和执行低效。

Conclusion: 确定了未来调度器必须考虑的关键特征，包括模型特定的卸载敏感性、工作负载特征、抢占和数据传输的成本结构，为设计能够高效管理异构多模型工作负载的下一代LLM服务系统提供了指导。

Abstract: Modern deployments of Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly require serving multiple models with diverse architectures, sizes, and specialization on shared, heterogeneous hardware. This setting introduces new challenges for resource allocation, dispatching, and scheduling, particularly under GPU memory constraints where partial CPU-GPU offloading and preemption become necessary. While existing systems primarily optimize throughput for a single model, comparatively little work addresses multi-model scheduling under these conditions. In this paper, we present an empirical study of how different LLMs behave across hardware platforms, focusing on the performance implications of layer offloading and preemption. We show that offloading leads to strongly non-linear and model-dependent degradation in decode throughput, with smaller models exhibiting sharper sensitivity to reduced GPU residency. We further demonstrate that preemption incurs substantial overhead, largely dominated by model state reload rather than key-value cache transfer, and that this cost varies significantly across models and hardware platforms. Additionally, we highlight the role of sequence length and interconnect bandwidth in amplifying data movement and execution inefficiencies. Based on these findings, we identify a set of key features that future schedulers must consider, including model-specific offloading sensitivity, workload characteristics, and the cost structure of preemption and data transfer. These insights provide guidance for the design of next-generation LLM serving systems capable of efficiently managing heterogeneous, multi-model workloads with hybrid CPU-GPU execution.

</details>


### [174] [EMO-BOOST: Emotion-Augmented Audio-Visual Features for Improved Generalization in Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19630)
*Aritra Marik,Marcel Klemt,Anna Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Emo-Boost是一个多模态深度伪造检测框架，通过融合传统的RGB-音频检测器和基于情感的检测器EmoForensics，利用情感作为高级语义线索来提高对未见过的深度伪造类型的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI模型的发展，新生成技术不断涌现，无法为每种篡改技术收集数据训练检测模型，因此对训练期间未见过的深度伪造类型进行泛化成为当前深度伪造检测研究的主要挑战。

Method: 提出Emo-Boost多模态框架，融合现成的RGB-音频检测器和基于情感的EmoForensics检测器。EmoForensics利用视觉和音频情感识别模块，并建模音频-视觉流中情感表示的内部和跨模态时间一致性。

Result: EmoForensics和传统低层次方法捕捉互补信号，结合这两种信号使Emo-Boost在FakeAVCeleb数据集上的跨篡改泛化平均AUC提升了2.1%。

Conclusion: 高级语义线索（如情感）能够支持低层次检测方法，提升对未见深度伪造类型的泛化能力。情感作为一种鲁棒的高级线索，与低层次特征相结合可产生协同效应，提高检测系统的泛化性能。

Abstract: With every advancement in generative AI models, forensics is under increasing pressure. The constant emergence of new generation techniques makes it impossible to collect data for each manipulation to train a deepfake detection model. Thus, generalizing to deepfakes unseen during training is one of the major challenges in current deepfake detection research. To tackle this challenge, we employ high-level semantic cues and argue that these cues can support low-level focused approaches in generalizing to unseen types of manipulations. In this work, we study emotions as a high-level semantic cue. We propose Emo-Boost, a multimodal deepfake detection framework that fuses an off-the-shelf RGB- and acoustic-focused deepfake detector with our emotion-based deepfake detector EmoForensics. EmoForensics utilises vision and audio emotion recognition modules and models intra- and inter-modal temporal consistency in emotion representations from an audio-visual stream. We found that EmoForensics and the low-level focused method capture complementary signals. Consequently, combining both signals in EmoBoost enhances the average cross-manipulation generalization AUC by 2.1% on FakeAVCeleb.

</details>


### [175] [Pseudocode-Guided Structured Reasoning for Automating Reliable Inference in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19663)
*Weicong Ni,Tianbao Jiang,Linlin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PStar：一个伪代码引导的结构化推理框架，通过自适应选择推理路径来减少视觉语言模型的幻觉，在POPE和MMStar基准上实现SOTA性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在机器人自动化中的高层推理方面表现出色，但其幻觉问题导致决策失败，带来安全和可靠性风险。现实世界任务的开放性和多样性要求更稳健、自适应的推理策略。

Method: 提出PStar框架：1)设计抽象推理函数和结构化伪代码库表示模块化推理策略；2)引入难度特征向量评估问题复杂度；3)自适应选择合适推理策略实现逐步推理。

Result: PStar显著降低幻觉率，在POPE基准上达到87.1%，在MMStar基准上达到68.0%，优于GPT-4V等现有方法，实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: PStar通过提供验证机制减少视觉语言错误，为在现实世界自动系统中部署更可信赖和确定性的视觉语言模型迈出关键一步，这些错误可能导致灾难性后果。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are becoming the cornerstone of high-level reasoning for robotic automation, enabling robots to parse natural language commands and perceive their environments. However, their susceptibility to hallucinations introduces critical failures in decision-making, posing significant safety and reliability risks in physical deployments. This challenge is exacerbated by the open-ended nature of real-world tasks, where questions vary vastly in difficulty and modality, demanding robust and adaptable reasoning strategies. To tackle this, we propose the Pseudocode-guided Structured Reasoning framework (PStar), which adaptively selects structured pseudocode reasoning paths to help VLMs perform flexible and step-by-step reasoning. We first design a set of abstract reasoning functions and formulate a structured pseudocode library to represent modular reasoning strategies. Crucially, we design a Difficulty Feature Vector (DFV) that allows the model to assess question complexity and adaptively choose appropriate reasoning strategies-enhancing robustness and interpretability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PStar significantly reduces hallucination rates, achieving state-of-the-art scores of 87.1% on POPE and 68.0% on MMStar, outperforming even GPT-4V. By providing a validated mechanism to reduce visual-language errors, PStar offers a critical step toward deploying more trustworthy and deterministic VLMs for real-world automated systems, where such errors can lead to catastrophic outcomes.

</details>


### [176] [Transforming Constraint Programs to Input for Local Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19671)
*Jo Devriendt,Patrick De Causmaecker,Marc Denecker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究建立了约束优化问题对称性与局部搜索邻域之间的联系，基于此可自动从IDP系统的约束规约中生成邻域，并在六个经典优化问题上验证了该技术的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将局部搜索算法应用于组合优化问题通常需要人工干预，将约束编译为元启发式算法的输入数据。这种手工编译过程耗时且容易出错，需要自动化方法来解决这个问题。

Method: 建立约束优化问题的对称性属性与局部搜索邻域之间的理论联系，利用这一联系在IDP系统中从约束规约自动生成邻域，无需人工干预。

Result: 在六个经典优化问题上评估了自动生成的邻域，实验结果支持该技术的可行性，表明通过对称性分析自动生成邻域是有效的方法。

Conclusion: 通过利用约束优化问题的对称性属性，可以自动化地从约束规约生成局部搜索邻域，这减少了人工干预需求，为组合优化问题提供了更高效的问题求解方法。

Abstract: Applying local search algorithms to combinatorial optimization problems is not an easy feat. Typically, human intervention is required to compile the constraints to input data for some metaheuristic algorithm. In this paper, we establish a link between symmetry properties of constraint optimization problems and local search neighborhoods, and we use this link to automatically generate neighborhoods from a constraint specification in the context of the IDP system. We evaluate the obtained neighborhoods for six classical optimization problems. The resulting observations support the viability of this technique.

</details>


### [177] [Projecting Latent RL Actions: Towards Generalizable and Scalable Graph Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19721)
*Franco Terranova,Guillermo Bernardez,Albert Cabellos-Aparicio,Nina Miolane,Abdelkader Lahmadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出投影智能体方法，通过连续GNN嵌入空间解决图组合优化问题，实现快速推理和更好的泛化能力，并发布LaGCO-RL库


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RL和GNN的图组合优化方法面临两个主要问题：1）在不同图实例间的泛化能力有限；2）随着动作空间增长，计算可扩展性差。需要同时解决这两个挑战。

Method: 提出投影智能体方法，直接在连续的GNN动作嵌入空间中操作：1）通过单次前向传播预测期望的潜在动作；2）使用简单的最近邻解码将潜在动作解码为有效的离散动作；3）建立共享的嵌入空间用于观察和动作，实现RL方法的公平比较。

Result: 在多样化基准测试中：1）推理速度比现有解决方案快16.2倍；2）泛化性能提高40%；3）仅使用简单的最近邻解码；4）为具有多个相互依赖变量的超线性决策空间开启了强RL性能的可能性。

Conclusion: 投影智能体方法通过连续潜在动作空间有效解决了图组合优化中的泛化和可扩展性问题。发布的LaGCO-RL库自动化了潜在动作空间构建，支持现有RL-GCO解决方案，促进了可重现性和对新基准的适应性。

Abstract: Graph combinatorial optimization (GCO) has attracted growing interest, as many NP-hard problems naturally admit graph formulations, yet their combinatorial explosion renders exact methods computationally intractable. Recent advances in Reinforcement Learning (RL) combined with Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have significantly improved learning-based GCO solvers. However, existing approaches face limitations in both generalization across diverse graph instances and computational scalability as action spaces grow. To address both challenges, we introduce projection agents, a novel RL-GCO approach that operates directly in a continuous GNN-based action embedding space, predicting a desired latent action in a single forward pass and subsequently decoding it into a valid discrete action. Additionally, we enable fair comparison across RL methods through a shared embedding space for both observations and actions. Across diverse benchmarks, our approach achieves up to 16.2x faster inference and up to 40% better generalization than existing solutions using only simple nearest-neighbor decoding, while opening the door to strong RL performance in super-linear decision spaces with multiple interdependent variables. Finally, we release LaGCO-RL, a Python library that automates latent action-space construction and supports existing RL-GCO solutions, promoting reproducibility and adaptation to new GCO benchmarks.

</details>


### [178] [EngiAI: A Multi-Agent Framework and Benchmark Suite for LLM-Driven Engineering Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19743)
*Gioele Molinari,Florian Felten,Soheyl Massoudi,Mark Fuge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了EngiBench基准套件用于评估LLM智能体在工程设计任务中的性能，并开发了EngiAI多智能体系统作为参考实现。基准包括工作流、RAG和HPC三个维度，测试结果显示专用模型表现优于开源模型，条件分支任务最具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架未能充分处理结合仿真、检索和制造准备的多智能体系统工程设计任务。当前缺乏系统化的评估方法来衡量LLM智能体在复杂工程设计工作流中的综合能力，特别是在需要协调多个专业化智能体的场景下。

Method: 1. 提出EngiBench基准套件，包含三个评估维度：工作流基准（7种提示风格）、RAG基准（门控评分）和HPC基准（端到端ML训练编排）。2. 开发EngiAI多智能体系统参考实现，基于LangGraph构建，协调7个专业化智能体（拓扑优化、文档检索、HPC作业编排、3D打印机控制等）通过监督者架构工作。

Result: 在4个LLM后端和2个EngiBench问题上：专用模型在Beams2D上平均任务完成率达96-97%，开源4B参数模型为55-78%；条件分支任务最具挑战性，在Photonics2D上完成率降至20-53%；RAG门控评分显示检索增强得分接近完美（≈1.0），无检索时接近零；HPC编排中，一个模型在100%运行中完成所有步骤，另一个降至50%，表明多步骤指令跟随在长工作流中会退化。

Conclusion: EngiBench基准套件和EngiAI参考实现填补了LLM多智能体系统工程设计评估的空白。研究结果表明LLM智能体在复杂工程设计工作流中具有潜力但面临挑战，特别是条件推理和长流程指令跟随能力需要提升。该框架为系统化评估和提升LLM工程设计能力提供了基础。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to engineering design tasks, yet existing evaluation frameworks do not adequately address multi-agent systems that combine simulation, retrieval, and manufacturing preparation. We introduce a benchmark suite with three evaluation dimensions: (1) a workflow benchmark with seven prompt styles targeting distinct cognitive demands-including direct tool use, semantic disambiguation, conditional branching, and working-memory tasks; (2) a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) benchmark with gated scoring isolating retrieval contributions to parameter selection; and (3) an High Performance Computing (HPC) benchmark evaluating end-to-end ML training orchestration on a SLURM cluster. Alongside the benchmark we present EngiAI, a Multi-Agent System (MAS) reference implementation built on LangGraph that operationalizes the benchmark by coordinating seven specialized agents through a supervisor architecture, unifying topology optimization, document retrieval, HPC job orchestration, and 3D printer control. Across four LLM backends and two EngiBench problems, proprietary models achieve 96-97% average task completion on Beams2D, while open-source 4B-parameter models reach 55-78%, with clear generational improvement. Conditional branching proves most challenging, with task completion dropping to 20-53% for the conditional style on Photonics2D. RAG gating confirms near-perfect retrieval-augmented scores ($\approx 1.0$) versus near-zero without retrieval, validating the evaluation design. On HPC orchestration, one model completes all pipeline steps in 100% of runs while another drops to 50%, revealing that multi-step instruction following degrades over long-running workflows.

</details>


### [179] [What Really Improves Mathematical Reasoning: Structured Reasoning Signals Beyond Pure Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19762)
*Yuze Zhao,Junpeng Fang,Lu Yu,Zhenya Huang,Kai Zhang,Qing Cui,Qi Liu,Jun Zhou,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明：代码在语言模型训练中主要提升编程能力而非通用推理能力，推理能力的提升更多源于跨域结构化推理轨迹（如代码-文本混合）而非纯可执行代码；增加数学推理样本密度可显著提升数学推理能力同时保持编程性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码已成为现代基础语言模型训练的标准组件，但其在编程之外的作用仍不清楚。研究旨在重新审视"代码提升推理能力"的主张，通过控制实验探究代码数据在语言模型训练中的具体作用和机制。

Method: 在10T-token语料库上进行控制预训练实验，实现细粒度的域分离。研究方法包括：1）限制代码数据为独立的可执行程序；2）控制代码-自然语言数据；3）调整数学推理样本密度；4）进行路由分析以观察专家激活模式。

Result: 1）代码主要提升编程能力而非通用推理能力，且与知识密集型任务（特别是复杂数学推理）存在竞争关系；2）常被归因于代码的推理增益更好地由跨域结构化推理轨迹解释；3）在固定数学预算内增加数学推理样本密度可显著提升困难数学推理能力，同时保持编程性能；4）路由分析显示数据组合效应反映在专家激活模式中。

Conclusion: 代码数据的作用具有选择性：主要增强编程能力，而通用推理能力的提升更多源于跨域结构化学习。研究结果阐明了数据特征在不同能力维度间的传递机制，为更精确的数据中心化优化策略提供了依据，认知支架可针对性缓解跨域权衡问题。

Abstract: Code has become a standard component of modern foundation language model (LM) training, yet its role beyond programming remains unclear. We revisit the claim that code improves reasoning through controlled pretraining experiments on a 10T-token corpus with fine-grained domain separation. Our findings are threefold. First, when code is restricted to standalone executable programs and Code-NL data are controlled for, code substantially improves programming ability but does not act as a general reasoning enhancer; instead, it competes with knowledge-intensive tasks, especially complex mathematical reasoning. Second, the reasoning gains often attributed to code are better explained by cross-domain structured reasoning traces, such as code-text and math-text mixtures, rather than by executable code alone. Third, increasing the density of structured math-domain samples within a fixed math budget yields substantial gains on difficult mathematical reasoning while largely preserving programming performance, suggesting that cognitive scaffolds offer a targeted way to mitigate cross-domain trade-offs. Finally, routing analyses show that data-composition effects are reflected in expert-activation patterns, providing mechanism-level evidence for competitive and synergistic interactions across domains. Our results clarify which data characteristics transfer across capability dimensions and point to more precise data-centric optimization strategies.

</details>


### [180] [GroupAffect-4: A Multimodal Dataset of Four-Person Collaborative Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19765)
*Meisam Jamshidi Seikavandi,Alice Modica,Anna Obara,Shan Ahmed Shaffi,Fabricio Batista Narcizo,Tanya Ignatenko,Ted Vucurevich,Karim Haddad,Daniel Barratt,Daniel Overholt,Jesper Bunsow Boldt,Paolo Burelli,Andrew Burke Dittberner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了GroupAffect-4多模态数据集，包含10个四人小组在四种生态任务中的交互数据，涵盖了生理、眼动、音频等多种信号，用于分析团体情境下的情感过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的情感计算、社交信号处理和会议语料库虽然捕捉了人类互动的重要方面，但很少支持将情感作为一个耦合的个体、人际和群体层面过程进行分析。所需的多模态信号（生理、眼动、音频等）通常分散在不同的数据集传统中。

Method: 创建了包含40名参与者（10个四人小组）的多模态语料库，每个小组完成四种生态协作任务：信息汇集、谈判、创意生成和公共物品游戏。每个参与者配备了腕戴式生理传感器、眼动追踪眼镜和近讲麦克风。数据包括连续情感自报告、任务后问卷、任务结果和大五人格分数，所有数据都与共享时钟时间对齐。

Result: 数据集覆盖了超过91%的预期生理数据窗口和98%的眼动追踪窗口，通过谈判区块的明显情感操作检查确认了强烈的任务效度。定义了15个可基准测试的目标，涵盖三个分析层面（个体内状态、个体间特质和群体动态），并报告了留一组交叉验证可行性基线，确立了数据集的评估范围。

Conclusion: GroupAffect-4数据集以BIDS启发式结构发布，包含Croissant元数据、数据表、每个会话的质量报告和开源处理脚本，为团体情境下的多层面情感过程分析提供了全面的研究资源。

Abstract: Existing affective-computing, social-signal-processing, and meeting corpora capture important parts of human interaction, but they rarely support analysis of affect in co-located groups as a coupled individual, interpersonal, and group-level process. The required signals (per-participant physiology, eye movement, audio, self-report, task outcomes, and personality) are usually fragmented across separate dataset traditions. We introduce GroupAffect-4, a multimodal corpus of 40 participants in 10 four-person groups, each completing four ecologically varied collaborative tasks spanning information pooling, negotiation, idea generation, and a public-goods game. Each participant is instrumented with a wrist-worn physiology sensor, eye-tracking glasses, and a close-talk microphone; sessions include continuous affect self-reports, post-task questionnaires, task outcomes, and Big-Five personality scores, all time-aligned to a shared clock. The dataset covers over 91% of expected physiology windows and 98% of eye-tracking windows, with strong task validity confirmed by a clear affective manipulation check across the negotiation block. We define fifteen benchmarkable targets spanning three analysis levels -- within-person state, between-person traits, and group dynamics -- and report leave-one-group-out feasibility baselines establishing the dataset's evaluative scope. GroupAffect-4 is released with a BIDS-inspired structure, Croissant metadata, a datasheet, per-session quality reports, and open processing scripts. Code and processing scripts are available at https://github.com/meisamjam/GroupAffect-4; the dataset is publicly archived at https://zenodo.org/records/20037847.

</details>


### [181] [Minimax Optimal Variance-Aware Regret Bounds for Multinomial Logistic MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19768)
*Pierre Boudart,Pierre Gaillard,Alessandro Rudi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了针对具有多项逻辑(MNL)混合模型的马尔可夫决策过程(MDPs)的强化学习算法，首次实现了与问题相关的极小极大最优遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对MNL混合MDPs的算法产生了$\tilde{O}(dH^2\sqrt{T})$的遗憾界，但该界在问题结构良好时可能过于保守。作者受到逻辑bandit文献的启发，希望引入问题相关的常数来改进遗憾界，特别是在结构化MDPs上获得更好的性能。

Method: 引入问题相关的常数$\bar{\sigma}_T \leq 1/2$，衡量学习者轨迹上最优下游值函数的归一化平均方差。基于此提出新的算法，其遗憾界为$\tilde{O}(dH^2\bar{\sigma}_T\sqrt{T})$，在$\bar{\sigma}_T=1/2$时恢复到最坏情况边界，在结构化MDPs上得到改进。

Result: 1. 算法实现了$\tilde{O}(dH^2\bar{\sigma}_T\sqrt{T})$的遗憾界，对KL约束的鲁棒MDPs，$\bar{\sigma}_T = O(H^{-1})$，将horizon依赖减少了一个因子$H$。2. 建立了匹配的下界$\Omega(dH^2\bar{\sigma}_T\sqrt{T})$，证明了（在对数因子内）的极小极大最优性。3. 首次完全刻画了MNL混合MDPs的遗憾复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作通过引入问题相关的方差度量$\bar{\sigma}_T$，首次为MNL混合MDPs实现了极小极大最优的强化学习算法，显著改进了现有结果，特别是在结构化MDPs上减少了horizon依赖。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning for episodic Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) whose transitions are modelled by a multinomial logistic (MNL) model. Existing algorithms for MNL mixture MDPs yield a regret of $\smash{\tilde{O}(dH^2\sqrt{T})}$ (Li et al., 2024), where $d$ is the feature dimension, $H$ the episode length, and $T$ the number of episodes. Inspired by the logistic bandit literature (Abeille et al., 2021; Faury et al., 2022; Boudart et al., 2026), we introduce a problem-dependent constant $\barσ\_T \leq 1/2$, measuring the normalised average variance of the optimal downstream value function along the learner's trajectory. We propose an algorithm achieving a regret of $\smash{\tilde{O}(dH^2\barσ\_T\sqrt{T})}$, which recovers the existing bound in the worst case and improves upon it for structured MDPs. For instance, for KL-constrained robust MDPs, $\barσ\_T = O(H^{-1})$, reducing the horizon dependence by a factor $H$. We further establish a matching $\smash{Ω(dH^2\barσ\_T\sqrt{T})}$ lower bound, proving minimax optimality (up to logarithmic factors) and fully characterising the regret complexity of MNL mixture MDPs for the first time.

</details>


### [182] [Prior Knowledge or Search? A Study of LLM Agents in Hardware-Aware Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19782)
*Dmitry Redko,Albert Fazlyev,Konstantin Sozykin,Maria Ivanova,Evgeny Burnaev,Egor Shvetsov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM在代码优化任务中高度依赖预训练先验知识而非提供的反馈或智能体结构，表现出贪婪优化特性，且在低密度语言环境中性能会下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM智能体结构日益复杂，难以评估哪些组件贡献最大，以及探索过程何时及如何失败，需要理解LLM在优化和发现任务中的行为特性。

Method: 通过三个受控实验：1) 纯黑盒优化中LLM行为分析；2) 零样本内核生成实验，测试输入尺寸信息的影响；3) 反馈循环内核优化实验，比较CUDA和TVM IR的性能差异。

Result: 1) LLM在纯黑盒优化中表现为贪婪优化器；2) 零样本内核生成中，输入尺寸信息无显著影响，模型收敛到相同内核参数，非常规尺寸时性能急剧下降；3) 反馈循环中，CUDA单调改进而TVM IR性能下降，表明低密度语言环境下优化会退化。

Conclusion: LLM在代码优化任务中主要依赖预训练先验知识，而非环境反馈或智能体结构，当前方法无法有效利用反馈信息进行实质性改进。

Abstract: LLM discovery and optimization systems are increasingly applied across domains, implementing a common propose-evaluate-revise loop. Such optimization or discovery progresses via context conditioning on received feedback from an environment. However, as modern LLM agents are increasingly complex in their structure, it is difficult to evaluate which components contribute the most, and when and how this exploration may fail. We answer these questions through three controlled experiments. Our findings: (1) In pure black-box optimization, LLMs act as greedy optimizers. (2) In zero-shot kernel generation, providing explicit input-size information has no measurable effect, models converge to the same kernel parameters regardless of size or temperature, as though the size instruction were invisible. Moreover, when tasked to perform kernel optimization for uncommon kernel sizes, performance sharply degrades regardless of the language used. (3) In feedback-loop kernel optimization, CUDA improves monotonically under iterative feedback, while TVM IR actively degrades, which demonstrates that kernel optimization degrades when models operate with low-density language. Our results conclude that LLMs in code optimization tasks highly depend on pretrained priors rather than provided feedback or agentic structure.

</details>


### [183] [From Prompts to Pavement Through Time: Temporal Grounding in Agentic Scene-to-Plan Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19824)
*Ahmed Y. Gado,Omar Y. Goba,Alaa Hassanein,Catherine M. Elias,Ahmed Hussein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文研究了在自动驾驶系统中为LLM/LMM集成添加时间条件能否提高场景到规划推理的连贯性。通过三种时间集成逐步增加的规划器架构在BDD-X数据集上的评估，发现时间条件重塑了推理风格但未显著改善标准NLP正确性指标，但定性分析显示了对危险预测、稳定纠正行为和策略分化的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶系统中使用LLM/LMM集成进行高级场景解释和规划时，通常将时间作为次要属性处理，缺乏时间接地导致连续动作推理的不一致性，损害了安全性和可解释性。论文旨在探索通过智能体间通信中的时间条件是否能在不损害语义或逻辑一致性的情况下保持或增强连贯性。

Method: 研究者引入了三种时间集成逐步增加的规划器架构（从基础到高级时间条件）。在BDD-X数据集的精选子集上进行评估，使用语义、句法和逻辑指标。通过标准NLP正确性指标和定性分析相结合的方法进行评估。

Result: 结果显示时间条件虽然重塑了推理风格，但在标准NLP正确性指标上未产生统计学上的显著改善。然而，定性分析揭示了预测性危险推理、稳定的纠正行为以及Sentinel模型中的策略分化等积极特征。

Conclusion: 研究明确了基于提示的时间接地方法的局限性，并建立了首个用于时间场景到规划推理的经验基准。结果表明时间条件虽然改进了某些定性特征，但未在传统度量标准上带来显著提升，为未来研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Recent attempts to support high-level scene interpretation and planning in Autonomous Vehicles (AVs) using ensembles of Large Language Models (LLMs) and Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) continue to treat time as a secondary property. This lack of temporal grounding leads to inconsistencies in reasoning about continuous actions, undermining both safety and interpretability. This work explores whether temporal conditioning within inter-agent communication can preserve or enhance coherence without introducing degradation in semantic or logical consistency. To investigate this, we introduce three planner architectures with progressively increasing temporal integration and evaluate them on curated subsets of the BDD-X dataset using semantic, syntactic, and logical metrics. Results show that while temporal conditioning reshapes reasoning style, it yields no statistically significant improvements in standard NLP-based correctness metrics. However, qualitative analysis reveals predictive hazard reasoning, stable corrective behavior, and strategic divergence in the Sentinel. These findings clarify the limits of prompt-based temporal grounding and establish the first empirical benchmark for temporal scene-to-plan reasoning.

</details>


### [184] [PEEK: Context Map as an Orientation Cache for Long-Context LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19932)
*Zhuohan Gu,Qizheng Zhang,Omar Khattab,Samuel Madden*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PEEK系统通过维护小型恒定大小的上下文映射（context map）来存储可重用的方向性知识，帮助LLM智能体在处理重复外部上下文时更准确、高效地工作，显著提升性能并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在处理重复外部上下文（如文档语料库和代码仓库）时，通常只保存轨迹、原始材料访问或任务级策略，而缺乏对重复上下文本身的可重用方向性知识，如上下文内容、组织方式、有用实体、常量和模式等。

Method: PEEK系统引入上下文映射作为智能体提示中的小型恒定大小工件，通过可编程缓存策略维护，包含三个模块：1）Distiller从推理时信号中提取可迁移知识；2）Cartographer将其转换为结构化编辑；3）基于优先级的Evictor强制执行固定token预算。

Result: 在长上下文推理和信息聚合任务中，PEEK相比强基线提升6.3-34.0%，使用93-145个更少的迭代次数，成本比最先进的提示学习框架ACE降低1.7-5.8倍。在上下文学习中，解决率和评分准确率分别提升6.0-14.0%和7.8-12.1%，成本降低1.4倍。

Conclusion: 上下文映射能有效帮助长上下文LLM智能体与重复外部上下文进行更准确、高效的交互，这些优势在不同语言模型和智能体架构中具有通用性，包括OpenAI Codex等生产级编码智能体。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly operate over long and recurring external contexts, like document corpora and code repositories. Across invocations, existing approaches preserve either the agent's trajectory, passive access to raw material, or task-level strategies. None of them preserves what we argue is most needed for repeated same-context workloads: reusable orientation knowledge (e.g., what the context contains, how it is organized, and which entities, constants, and schemas have historically been useful) about the recurring context itself. We introduce PEEK, a system that caches and maintains this orientation knowledge as a context map: a small, constant-sized artifact in the agent's prompt that gives it a persistent peek into the external context. The map is maintained by a programmable cache policy with three modules: a Distiller that extracts transferable knowledge from inference-time signals, a Cartographer that translates it into structured edits, and a priority-based Evictor that enforces a fixed token budget. On long-context reasoning and information aggregation, PEEK improves over strong baselines by 6.3-34.0% while using 93-145 fewer iterations and incurring 1.7-5.8x lower cost than the state-of-the-art prompt-learning framework, ACE. On context learning, PEEK improves solving rate and rubric accuracy by 6.0-14.0% and 7.8-12.1%, respectively, at 1.4x lower cost than ACE. These gains generalize across LMs and agent architectures, including OpenAI Codex, a production-grade coding agent. Together, these results show that a context map helps long-context LLM agents interact with recurring external contexts more accurately and efficiently.

</details>


### [185] [Robotics-Inspired Guardrails for Foundation Models in Socially Sensitive Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19940)
*Rebecca Ramnauth,Drazen Brscic,Brian Scassellati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究人员将机器人学中的行为控制理论应用于大模型安全防护，提出Grounded Observer框架，通过运行时约束强制执行来防止对话轨迹偏离到危险状态，并在三个实际场景中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型被部署在教育、心理健康、照护等社会敏感领域，但现有的安全防护方法（如训练时对齐、提示工程、解码约束和事后审核）主要提供经验性风险降低而非可执行的行为保证，且大多将安全性视为单个输出的属性而非整个交互轨迹的属性。

Method: 作者借鉴机器人学在不确定闭环系统中约束执行的形式化概念，将安全防护重新定义为交互轨迹的运行时行为控制问题，并实例化为Grounded Observer框架。该框架能够实时干预以防止交互偏离到不良状态，同时适应不同的社会情境。

Result: 该框架在三个真实世界部署中进行了验证：闲聊对话、家庭自闭症治疗和学校行为降级管理。在所有场景中，该框架都实现了运行时干预，有效缓解了向不良交互状态的漂移，并能适应多样化的社会背景。

Conclusion: 将安全防护重新定义为交互轨迹的运行时行为控制是解决大模型在敏感应用中安全问题的有前景方向。Grounded Observer框架提供了比现有方法更强的行为保证，作者还讨论了框架扩展方向并提出实现更强保证的研究路径。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly deployed in socially sensitive domains such as education, mental health, and caregiving, where failures are often cumulative and context-dependent. Existing guardrail approaches -- ranging from training-time alignment to prompting, decoding constraints, and post-hoc moderation -- primarily provide empirical risk reduction rather than enforceable behavioral guarantees, and largely treat safety as a property of individual outputs rather than interaction trajectories. We reframe guardrails as a problem of runtime behavioral control over interaction trajectories, drawing on robotics to introduce formal constructs for constraint enforcement in uncertain, closed-loop systems. We instantiate these ideas in the Grounded Observer framework and apply it across three real-world deployments: small talk, in-home autism therapy, and behavioral de-escalation in schools. Across settings, the framework enables runtime interventions that mitigate drift into undesirable interaction regimes while adapting to diverse social contexts. We discuss extensions to the framework and propose research directions toward stronger guarantees.

</details>


### [186] [Probabilistic Tiny Recursive Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19943)
*Amin Sghaier,Ali Parviz,Alexia Jolicoeur-Martineau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Probabilistic TRM (PTRM) 在 Tiny Recursive Models 基础上引入高斯噪声注入的随机探索机制，无需重新训练即可通过并行轨迹探索不同解决方案，显著提升推理任务的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 Tiny Recursive Models (TRM) 虽然参数少但能解决复杂推理任务，但其确定性递归容易收敛到次优解且缺乏逃逸机制。现有方法依赖任务特定的输入扰动和投票聚合，缺乏任务无关的解决方案。

Method: PTRM 在每个深度递归步骤注入高斯噪声，生成多个并行轨迹以探索不同的解决方案盆地，并利用模型原有的 Q head（在原 TRM 中用于早停）来选择最佳解决方案。

Result: 在多个基准测试中实现显著准确率提升：Sudoku-Extreme 从 87.4% 提高到 98.75%，Pencil Puzzle Bench 从 62.6% 提高到 91.2%。在后者上，PTRM 以 7M 参数实现 91.2% 准确率，超越前沿 LLMs 的 55.1%，成本降低超过 10000 倍。

Conclusion: PTRM 提供了一个任务无关的测试时计算扩展框架，无需重新训练或任务特定增强即可通过随机探索显著提升 Tiny Recursive Models 的性能，在小模型上实现了超越大语言模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Tiny Recursive Models (TRM) solve complex reasoning tasks with a fraction of the parameters of modern large language models (LLMs) by iteratively refining a latent state and final answer. While powerful, their deterministic recursion can lead to convergence at suboptimal solutions, without escape mechanism. A common workaround relies on task-specific input perturbations at test time combined with answer aggregation via voting. We introduce Probabilistic TRM (PTRM), a task-agnostic framework for test-time compute scaling that addresses this limitation through stochastic exploration. PTRM injects Gaussian noise at each deep recursion step, enabling parallel trajectories to explore diverse solution basins, and selects among them using the model's existing Q head (used for early stopping in the original TRM). Without requiring retraining or task-specific augmentations, PTRM enables substantial accuracy gains across benchmarks, including Sudoku-Extreme (87.4% to 98.75%) and on various puzzles from Pencil Puzzle Bench (62.6% to 91.2%). On the latter, PTRM achieves nearly double the accuracy of frontier LLMs (91.2% vs. 55.1%) at less than 0.0001x the cost, using only 7M parameters.

</details>


### [187] [GeoX: Mastering Geospatial Reasoning Through Self-Play and Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20006)
*Kyeongjin Ahn,Seungeon Lee,Krishna P. Gummadi,Meeyoung Cha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GeoX是一个自博弈框架，通过可执行程序学习空间逻辑，无需大规模人工标注数据，显著提升基础视觉语言模型在空间推理任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间推理需要在复杂场景结构上解决基于图像的问题，但标注广阔组合性问题空间的成本高昂，阻碍了这种能力的发展。

Method: 提出了GeoX自博弈框架：使用单一多模态策略提出可执行程序作为空间问题，并通过演绎、归纳和反推三种推理模式解决这些问题。通过验证器执行程序产生奖励信号，使用强化学习联合优化两种角色。

Result: GeoX将基础视觉语言模型的性能平均提升5.5个百分点，与使用数百万人工标注数据训练的传统基线方法匹配甚至超越。同时发布了通过自博弈积累的地理空间理解基准。

Conclusion: 通过自博弈框架结合可执行程序和学习到的验证信号，可以有效发展空间逻辑能力，减少对大规模人工标注数据的依赖，为地理空间推理提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Geospatial reasoning requires solving image-grounded problems over the complex spatial structure of a scene. However, developing this capability is hindered by the cost of annotating a vast and combinatorial question space. We propose GeoX, a self-play framework that acquires spatial logic through executable programs that yield verifiable rewards, without relying on large-scale human-curated data Given a satellite or aerial image, our framework employs a single multimodal policy that proposes spatial problems as executable programs and solves them under three reasoning modes-abduction, deduction, and induction-over spatial primitives and an image understanding tool. A verifier executes each program to covert a reward signal that jointly optimizes the two roles via reinforcement learning. GeoX consistently improves its base VLMs by up to 5.5 points on average, matching or exceeding conventional baselines trained on millions of curated data. Along-side the proposed method, we release a benchmark for geospatial understanding accumulated through self-play.

</details>


### [188] [AutoResearchClaw: Self-Reinforcing Autonomous Research with Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20025)
*Jiaqi Liu,Shi Qiu,Mairui Li,Bingzhou Li,Haonian Ji,Siwei Han,Xinyu Ye,Peng Xia,Zihan Dong,Congyu Zhang,Letian Zhang,Guiming Chen,Haoqin Tu,Xinyu Yang,Lu Feng,Xujiang Zhao,Haifeng Chen,Jiawei Zhou,Xiao Wang,Weitong Zhang,Hongtu Zhu,Yun Li,Jieru Mei,Hongliang Fei,Jiaheng Zhang,Linjie Li,Linjun Zhang,Yuyin Zhou,Sheng Wang,Caiming Xiong,James Zou,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Mingyu Ding,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoResearchClaw是一个多智能体自主研究系统，通过结构化多智能体辩论、自我修复执行、可验证结果报告、人机协作和跨轮次演化等机制，实现迭代式科学研究，在ARC-Bench基准上比AI Scientist v2提升54.7%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主研究系统通常将科学研究建模为线性流程：依赖单智能体推理，执行失败时停止，并且不跨轮次积累经验。真实科学研究是迭代式的：假设从多个角度受到挑战，实验失败并指导下一次尝试，经验在多个循环中积累。

Method: 系统基于五个核心机制构建：1) 结构化多智能体辩论用于假设生成和结果分析；2) 自我修复执行器，采用Pivot/Refine决策循环将失败转化为信息；3) 可验证结果报告，防止伪造数字和幻觉引用；4) 人机协作，提供从完全自主到逐步监督的七种干预模式；5) 跨轮次演化，将过去的错误转化为未来的保障。

Result: 在包含25个主题的实验阶段基准ARC-Bench上，AutoResearchClaw比AI Scientist v2性能提升54.7%。人机协作消融实验显示，在关键决策点进行精准、有针对性的协作持续优于完全自主和详尽的逐步监督两种极端。

Conclusion: AutoResearchClaw被定位为研究放大器，旨在增强而非替代人类科学判断。系统展示了多智能体辩论、自适应执行和可验证报告如何协同工作以推动自主科学研究向前发展。

Abstract: Automating scientific discovery requires more than generating papers from ideas. Real research is iterative: hypotheses are challenged from multiple perspectives, experiments fail and inform the next attempt, and lessons accumulate across cycles. Existing autonomous research systems often model this process as a linear pipeline: they rely on single-agent reasoning, stop when execution fails, and do not carry experience across runs. We present AutoResearchClaw, a multi-agent autonomous research pipeline built on five mechanisms: structured multi-agent debate for hypothesis generation and result analysis, a self-healing executor with a \textsc{Pivot}/\textsc{Refine} decision loop that transforms failures into information, verifiable result reporting that prevents fabricated numbers and hallucinated citations, human-in-the-loop collaboration with seven intervention modes spanning full autonomy to step-by-step oversight, and cross-run evolution that converts past mistakes into future safeguards. On ARC-Bench, a 25-topic experiment-stage benchmark, AutoResearchClaw outperforms AI Scientist v2 by 54.7%. A human-in-the-loop ablation across seven intervention modes reveals that precise, targeted collaboration at high-leverage decision points consistently outperforms both full autonomy and exhaustive step-by-step oversight.
